Slices: **(1)** core — net-new own const `EVIDENCE_KINDS` (implemented|tested|explained|revisited; a DISTINCT axis from `EVIDENCE_STRENGTHS`, doc-commented + disjointness-tested so they are never conflated) + `mastery-evidence.ts` wire contracts (`EVIDENCE_ARTIFACT_URL_MAX_CHARS=2048`, `urlNoNul` NUL-reject, optional/nullable `artifactUrl`, `recordedOn` FORMAT-only here — default-today/reject-future are service-side, D7). **(2)** db schema + **migration 0012** (`mastery_evidence`; `user_id`+`exercise_id` CASCADE, `kind` CHECK, `artifact_url` nullable, `recorded_on` date NOT NULL, **NO UNIQUE** — a kind may recur; generated SQL glanced at a STOP-the-line before dependent slices, passed unchanged) + 4 constraint tests. **(3)** `mastery-evidence.repository` (create/find/delete + batched `listEvidenceByExerciseIds` for the embed + `hasRequiredEvidence` for the D1 gate + `countEvidenceByKind` for the D2 guard) + 6 integration tests; cross-module reads exported as **NARROW read-only interfaces** (`MasteryEvidenceGateRead`, `MasteryEvidenceEmbedRead`, `ExerciseOwnershipRead`) so read-only is type-enforced (review-seat required tightening). **(4)** api — new `modules/mastery-evidence/` (POST 201 / **DELETE 204**; ownership 404, future-date 400, D2 delete-guard 409) + the **D1 completion gate** folded into `exercises.service.updateStatus` (409 `EXERCISE_INCOMPLETE_EVIDENCE`, order 404→409→write) + the **D4 embed** in `GET /learning-plans/:id` (each exercise carries `evidence[]`, one batched query, no N+1) + `app.ts` wiring (one shared repo, three narrowed consumers); OpenAPI regenerated **31 → 33 paths** (drift gate green). **(5)** route tests + docs. **D1 (the teeth):** an exercise cannot be `complete` without ≥1 implemented AND ≥1 tested evidence — a cross-table SERVICE precondition (Postgres cannot CHECK across tables), the M3-02 D1 pattern inverted. **D2 (airtight):** deleting the LAST implemented/tested evidence of a `complete` exercise is refused, so the gate is a true always-invariant. **Demonstrated detection (gate-modification law):** TWO planted-FAILs proven RED then reverted on fictional data — neutering the D1 gate turned the no-evidence-complete 409 red (got 200), neutering the D2 guard turned the last-tested-delete 409 red (got 204); allow-case tests (non-last / explained / revisited deletes → 204) and the D1 positive path (complete WITH evidence → 200) prove neither over-blocks. **GATES:** `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; `pnpm test` green (constraint + repo + route suites incl. the OpenAPI drift gate). ### M3-05 · Revisit scheduling · **S** · `done`

- Completed exercises enter a spaced review queue (e.g., 7/30/90 days); `GET /review-queue` lists due revisits; completing a revisit records `revisited` evidence.

*(2026-07-25 BUILD RECORD, one PR, four reviewable slices — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **SCHEMA-ONLY, NO LLM** (deterministic date math — no new prompt / adversarial corpus / live pass; **NO new ADR** — ADR-0003/0007 + the ADR-0013 family cover it; the M3-02/M3-03 class). Slices: **(1)** core — `revisit.ts`: `REVISIT_INTERVALS_DAYS [7,30,90]`, `computeRevisitState` (ROLLING ladder — interval k measured from the LAST counted revisit; k≥3 → GRADUATED, leaves the queue forever; **STRICT `recorded_on > completed_on` epoch filter** — reject-future + restamp-today make epoch resets airtight with zero extra state, and a same-day revisit of a fresh completion deliberately does not count), local Hinnant `dayNumber`/`civilFromDays` pair (core cannot import scoring), review-queue wire contracts (`completedOn` deliberately NOT on the Exercise wire shape); 18 table-driven unit tests. **Demonstrated detection #1:** inverting the ladder to [90,30,7] turned exactly 9/18 RED, reverted. **(2)** db — `exercises.completed_on` (nullable date, string-mode) + `exercises_completed_on_check` `CHECK ((status='complete') = (completed_on IS NOT NULL))` (single-table, ZERO bypass — `updateExerciseStatus` is the sole status mutator, creates default `planned`) + **migration 0014, the repo's FIRST hand-edited SQL**: backfill `UPDATE … SET completed_on = updated_at::date WHERE status='complete'` inserted between the generated ADD COLUMN and ADD CONSTRAINT (a never-LATE bound; `::date` truncates in the session timezone so a legacy revisit can surface at most ~1 day early — stated in the SQL comment); glanced at a STOP-the-line on the committed blob, PASS (em-dash in the hand-edit comment ruled house style). Repo: `updateExerciseStatus` gains `completedOn`; net-new `listCompletedExercises` + narrow `ExerciseReviewRead`. Service stamping RODE IN THIS COMMIT (accepted at the glance: splitting would leave a red intermediate once the CHECK applies): stamp server-local today on transition INTO complete, PRESERVE on idempotent complete→complete (epoch stability — pinned by a two-clock two-instance route test), clear on exit, restamp on re-completion (new epoch); ### M3-06 · Evidence → profile upgrades · **S** · `done`

- When an exercise completes with full evidence, the linked `profile_skills` level upgrade is **suggested** and applied only on Carlos's confirmation; the audit trail (which evidence justified which upgrade) is preserved.
- PARKED design constraints from the M0-08 importer review (2026-07-14), to resolve before this story starts: (1) **field ownership** — the importer owns `profile_skills.level` today and its full-sync mirror overwrites/deletes freely; a declared_level / earned_level / effective_level split is a CANDIDATE only, not a decision. (2) **Full-sync must never destroy or orphan mastery evidence** — an upgrade earned here has no markdown home, so a re-import would silently revert it (and cascade-deleting a skill row would orphan its evidence) unless ownership is resolved first. (3) This story introduces the first **second writer** to profile tables — it must route through the parser's shared normalization (see the M0-08b schema park). (4) **Downgrade/correction semantics** are unspecified: evidence goes stale and upgrades can be wrong; the audit trail needs a story for reversals, not just grants.

*(2026-07-25 BUILD RECORD, one PR, five reviewable slices — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **SCHEMA + SERVICE, NO LLM** (deterministic suggest-and-confirm — no prompt / adversarial corpus / live pass / spend). **NEW ADR-0014** (the FIRST M3 story to own one): it formally narrows a ratified contract ("markdown is the single source of truth" → "…for the DECLARED level; effective is a computed view") and resolves four parks across two subsystems — a "new major technical choice", unlike the M3-03/M3-05 struck-ADR class. **The four M0-08 parks, RESOLVED (ADR-0014):** (1) **ownership = table-plus-projection** — `profile_skills.level` stays DECLARED, importer-owned, untouched; an active `skill_upgrades` row is EARNED (`to_level`, always `solid`); EFFECTIVE = `maxSkillLevel(declared, …active earned)` computed in `getProfile`, NEVER stored (`max`, not "earned overrides" — a later declared promotion is never capped). The three-column split was rejected (doesn't remove the audit tables; entangles the importer's idempotency). (2) **re-import cannot revert/orphan (structural)** — the importer never touches the new tables; `profile_skill_id`/`exercise_id`/`mastery_evidence_id` are ON DELETE SET NULL onto durable snapshot columns (M2-10 precedent). (3) **park 3 = one shared normalization** — core `skillNameKey = lower(name)` (NOT trim+lower); `syncProfile`'s three skill dedup sites refactored to it, the upgrade writer keys off a stored row, so the "second writer to `profile_skills`" never materializes. (4) **park 4 = append-only + revoke** — `status ∈ {active, revoked}`, never deleted; a partial unique index `(user_id, skill_name_key) WHERE status='active'` backstops one-active-grant (23505 → 409, race-only). Slices: **(1)** core — `skillNameKey`, `SKILL_LEVEL_RANK`/`maxSkillLevel`, `UPGRADE_STATUSES`, wire schemas; `profileSkillWithDeclaredSchema` kept SEPARATE from the base `profileSkillSchema` so the fit engine strips `declaredLevel` (OD-7 realized — plan-literal in-place edit would have churned every scoring/api fixture; recorded implementer's structural choice). **Detection #1:** inverting `maxSkillLevel` (max→min) turned the 4 elevation cases RED incl. declared-expert-not-suppressed; reverted (18 tests). **(2)** scoring — pure `suggestSkillUpgrades` + the sole OD-3 predicate `hasFullMasteryEvidence` (≥1 implemented+tested+explained; `revisited` excluded); first read-only borrower of `matching.ts` outside the fit engine (fork-if-loosened noted); target always `solid` (OD-4). **Detection #2:** dropping `explained` from the predicate turned the no-suggestion-without-explained test RED; reverted (10 tests). **(3)** db — schema file + **migration 0015 (two additive tables, NO backfill ⇒ NO hand-edit)** routed to the review seat at a STOP-the-line glance on the committed blob (PASS, corroborated firsthand); dev-migrate proof (ledger row 16, partial index + CHECKs + FK matrix verified via `\d`); `SkillUpgradesRepository` (grant-with-evidence tx, list/find, race-safe revoke) + `getProfile` overlay + `skillNameKey` refactor. **Detection #3:** dropping the partial unique index on the test DB turned the duplicate-active test RED; restored, verified (9 repo tests incl. the **park-2 proof** — identical re-import all-zero + effective solid; declared→expert = effective max; rename → detached; **pure deletion → grant+snapshots persist, detached, drops from getProfile**, the review-seat-required leg). **(4)** api — `modules/skill-upgrades/` (routes + service + its repo; narrow reads for exercises/mastery/gaps/profile) wired in app.ts; server re-derives every confirm (404 skill/exercise before 409 not-derivable; 23505→409 already-active is race-only, unit-tested); GET /profile now serves effective `level` + `declaredLevel`; OpenAPI **36 → 39 paths** (drift green). **Detections #4/#5:** neutering server re-derivation → non-matching POST 201-instead-of-409 RED; dropping `status='active'` from the overlay → post-revoke effective stays solid RED. Both reverted (14 route + 2 service tests). **(5)** docs — ADR-0014 + ARCHITECTURE ERD/M3-06 addendum + Accelerator & Profile surface rows + this record + the **M3-06a Icebox park** (below). **GATES:** `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean (CI view — the sole local warn is gitignored `.claude/settings.local.json`, absent from a clean checkout); root `pnpm test` **1279 passing** (up from 1226; M1-07 injection corpus green throughout). Per-artifact NUL/C0 scan clean on every committed blob (PIPE, never capture); **the Write-tool raw-U+0000 hazard struck TWICE in slice 1** (a guard literal and a test string) — both caught by pre-commit byte inspection and Python/`perl`-fixed to `\u0000` before the commit, zero committed strikes. `privacy-check` content leg **PASS** — run after the final commit, before push, per the profile-adjacent trigger (this branch touched `profile.repository.ts`'s overlay + `profile.routes.ts`): 112 distinctive real-profile tokens checked against the branch diff, zero strings; all fixtures fictional Alex Rivera. Slice-3a lint gap self-corrected in slice 5: drizzle-generated 0015 meta JSON was prettier-nonconformant (per-slice scoped checks missed it; bare `pnpm lint` caught it) — reformatted, reviewed SQL blob byte-identical. **NEXT for close:** pre-merge class-(a) glance on the final head (scoring/repo/route/test + OpenAPI regen), then the merge word + CAS `--match-head-commit` + SEAL.*

SEAL corroborated FIRSTHAND by the review seat on main `21a03e6`: merge tree **tree-identical** to the reviewed head `03cece2` (`git diff --quiet` exit 0 — the merge introduced nothing beyond the reviewed head); true 2-parent `--merge` topology with `216de96` and `03cece2` both ancestor-verified; PR #66 branch deleted local + remote; remote heads = `main` only, 0 tags; working tree clean; local main == origin/main == `21a03e6`. ## M4 — Integrations & Hardening (Week 12)

### M4-01 · Exercise → case-study draft · **M** · `done`

- A completed exercise with evidence can generate a case-study draft pre-filled with the template sections, linked artifacts, and *personal / AI-assisted* provenance; publishing remains a manual portfolio-content step.

*(2026-07-25 BUILD RECORD, one PR, four reviewable slices — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **SCHEMA + SERVICE, NO LLM** (deterministic template assembly — no prompt / corpus / run table / live pass / spend; the M3-06 class). **NO NEW ADR (OD-7):** every pattern is shipped precedent (ADR-0010 owns the target grammar; deterministic renderer + stored snapshot + manual export = M2-10/M2-12; SET-NULL navigation FK + snapshots = M3-06/ADR-0014; CAS-event verb = M1-10 lineage) — no ratified contract is narrowed (the ADR-0014 test), so decisions live in the ERD/API addenda + this record (the M3-03/M3-05 struck-ADR precedent). **The OD-7 divergence note (merge condition), stated:** case-study drafting is DETERMINISTIC while ADR-0013 learning-plan/interview drafting is LLM because every AC field (seven sections, linked-artifact lines, provenance label) is **row-derivable** from `exercises`+`mastery_evidence`+`exercise_gaps`; the only prose an LLM would add is the case study's actual argument, which by ADR-0010 + the honesty invariant MUST be Carlos's own voice — so the draft ships **TODO scaffolding that instructs, never generated claims** (H-01). A drafting LLM would produce exactly the sentences a human must author, buying nothing and risking fabrication (full rationale in the ARCHITECTURE M4-01 ERD addendum). Slices: **(1)** core — `CASE_STUDY_STATUSES` (its own const, `draft|published` portfolio-lifecycle terminal, NOT the review family) + `EXERCISE_CASE_STUDY_PROVENANCES` (personal subset, `satisfies`-guarded; professional excluded at the wire, OD-3), wire schemas (`createCaseStudyBodySchema` title-optional/NUL-guarded), and the pure clock-free renderer `renderCaseStudyDraftMarkdown` — emits EXACTLY the ADR-0010 grammar by construction; interior-newline collapse is the single load-bearing render guard; posting text BANNED (linked gaps → COUNT only, T5). **Planted-FAILs:** dropping a `##` section → section-count RED; removing the collapse → injection matrix RED (both reverted; 32 tests incl. an 11-case injection matrix). **(2)** db — `case_studies` (one table) + **migration 0016 (CREATE TABLE only, NO backfill ⇒ NO hand-edit)** routed to a STOP-the-line glance on the committed blob (PASS, cleared by the review seat); `user_id` CASCADE + `exercise_id` SET NULL onto durable `exercise_title`/`rendered_markdown` snapshots; two CHECKs (provenance admits the full ERD vocabulary, status `draft|published`); `UNIQUE(exercise_id)` enforcing the ERD zero-or-one edge (NULLs distinct → orphans never block a fresh draft); `CaseStudiesRepository` (create / findByExerciseId / find / list / race-safe `refreshDraft` WHERE status='draft' / one-way `publishCaseStudy` CAS / delete-any-status) + `ExerciseCaseStudyRead` narrow view. **Planted-FAIL:** dropped the provenance CHECK on the live test DB → the raw-SQL `'bogus'` insert returned `rowCount 1` instead of 23514, proving the DB constraint (not drizzle typing) is the guard; restored (14 real-Postgres tests incl. SET-NULL durability + user cascade). **(3)** api — `modules/case-studies/` (routes + service + its repo; narrow reads for exercises/mastery) wired in app.ts; six routes: POST create/refresh (OD-1 full-replacement, 201/200), GET list (markdown omitted) + GET :id, GET :id/export (raw `text/markdown`, no status gate — OD-5), POST :id/publish (one-way CAS, OD-2), DELETE :id (any status, OD-4). Server re-derives the exercise's completion status (never trusts the client). A comment-correctness fix, no behavior change. **GATES (bare, per slice):** `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean (CI view — the sole local warn is gitignored `.claude/settings.local.json`, absent from a clean checkout); root `pnpm test` **1342 passing** (up from 1279: +32 core, +14 db, +17 api). Per-artifact NUL/C0 scan clean on every committed blob (PIPE, never capture); **the Write-tool raw-U+0000 hazard struck ONCE in slice 1** (the `noNul` guard literal) and once in a slice-1 test string — both caught by pre-commit byte inspection and `perl`-fixed to `\u0000`, zero committed strikes. `privacy-check` content leg run after the final commit, before push. **M3-06a** (evidence-recency bound on suggestions) stays in the Icebox — untouched. **NEXT for close:** consolidated class-(a) glance on S1 core + the final head (S1's early-glance was skipped, so S1+S3 get one review), then the merge word + CAS `--match-head-commit` + SEAL.

### M4-02 · Outcomes → matching feedback · **M** · `done`

- Application outcomes (screens, rejections, offers) produce **suggested** adjustments to search-criteria weights (e.g., dimensions that correlate with progression); suggestions are shown with their supporting data and applied only on confirmation — the loop closes with a human in it.

*(2026-07-26 BUILD RECORD, one PR, four reviewable slices — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **SCHEMA + SERVICE + PURE ENGINE, NO LLM** (the M3-06/M4-01 class — no prompt / corpus / run table / live pass / spend). **NO NEW ADR:** every pattern is shipped precedent (recompute-not-store = the review-queue projection; server-re-derive-before-write = M4-01 CONDITION-#1; CAS-pinned confirm = PUT /criteria; read-only `matching.ts` borrow = M3-06; append-only audit = resume_variants) — no ratified contract narrowed, so decisions live in the ERD/API addenda + this record. **The defining interpretive move (OD-1, gate-ratified):** `search_criteria` has NO numeric weights, so the AC's "weight adjustments" honestly translates to **removal-only edits over author-owned slugs** — never invents vocabulary, never touches `hardFilters` (the M1-08 cap-never-exclude law). **The first flywheel edge closed.** Slices: **(1)** core + scoring — `CRITERIA_ADJUSTMENT_KINDS` (two removals) + structured-only wire schemas (category-iff-positive superRefine; **no free-text field** — a schema-pin test) + the ONE `applyCriteriaAdjustment` (undefined on absent-slug or min(1) emptying); the shared `formatStageChangeDetail`/`parseStageChangeDetail` pair + stage rank ladder in core (`applications.service` refactored to the formatter; the detail arrow is a `→` escape, source-byte law); the pure `suggestCriteriaAdjustments` 2×2 engine — cohort classification with every excluded population disclosed, integer-only rate comparison (cross-multiplication, no floats), `MIN_RESOLVED_ANALYZABLE` gate, determinism ordering. **Planted-FAILs:** neutering the gate flips below-threshold status `insufficient_data`→`ok` RED; dropping the applier min(1) guard lets an emptied list escape as invalid criteria RED (both reverted; 22 tests). **(2)** db — `criteria_adjustments` (one table) + **migration 0017 (CREATE TABLE only, zero rows ⇒ db:generate only, NO hand-edit)**; `user_id` CASCADE, kind/category CHECKs + the boolean-equality kind↔category law; jsonb `evidence` (ids/company/title/stages/counts only — **NO requirement text**, the skill_upgrades privacy precedent) + DB-only `criteria_before/after`; **NO unique index, NO revoke verb** (convergence natural; undo = ordinary PUT /criteria); `confirmAdjustment` = the search_criteria CAS + audit insert in ONE tx (CAS first ⇒ a stale pin writes ZERO audit rows), reusing the exported `updatedAtMatches`/`DB_NOW` (one CAS, one clock); narrow reads `applications.listStageChangeEvents` + `extractions.listEligibleRequirementTexts`. **Planted-FAIL:** dropped the category↔kind CHECK on the live test DB → a categoryless-positive raw insert returned `rowCount 1` instead of 23514, proving the DB is the guard; restored. Migration 0017 verified applied to the dev DB (table + 3 CHECKs + FK). 6 real-Postgres tests (constraints + tx atomicity incl. the zero-audit-rows-on-conflict pin). **(3)** api — `modules/criteria-adjustments/` (routes + service; narrow reads for applications/extractions/criteria) wired in app.ts (the applications repository hoisted to a shared const — two consumers). GET /criteria-suggestions (200 always; `criteriaUpdatedAt` rides as the confirm pin), POST /criteria-adjustments (400 → 404 `CRITERIA_NOT_FOUND` → server RE-DERIVES the full list → 409 `SUGGESTION_NOT_DERIVABLE` → CAS apply → 409 `STALE_CRITERIA` → 201), GET /criteria-adjustments (audit list). Logs carry counts + ids + the closed kind enum ONLY. OpenAPI **43 → 45 paths** (drift green). **DEVIATION (disclosed):** the plan said 46 — but GET+POST `/criteria-adjustments` share ONE OpenAPI path key, so three operations add only two path keys; the plan counted operations, the honest number is 45. Public allowlist unchanged (all routes guarded). **Planted-FAILs:** OpenAPI drift RED (restored the base 43-path spec) → green after regen; **HEADLINE** — neutering the server re-derivation made a tampered `typescript` removal return 201 instead of 409 (the M4-01 CONDITION-#1 "never trust the client" lineage), observed RED then reverted. 10 route tests (401×3, 403 CSRF, 400, 404-before-409, tampered 409, stale-pin 409, happy 201 incl. audit-row-present + criteria-changed + pin-advanced + suggestion-vanishes, insufficient_data, audit list). **(4)** web + docs — `pages/criteria/index.vue` ("Criteria tuning", linked from applications index): observation-voice cards ("a description of N outcomes, not a cause") with escaped evidence rows + explicit Apply button + past-adjustments list; either 409 → "state changed — refreshed" + refetch (4 component tests). ARCHITECTURE ERD `criteria_adjustments` block + M4-02 addendum (records OD-1 weights→removal-only, the two deliberate M3-06 deviations, the phraseMatches stopword residual disposition, stage-detail-format now load-bearing) + the flywheel first-edge marked BUILT + the Criteria-tuning API row + this record. **RATIFIED residual (accept + document, this review, per "every finding gets a disposition"):** the borrowed `phraseMatches` has no stopword list, so a one-token slug (`go`, `r`, `ai`) over-matches incidental requirement text — and UNLIKE M3-06 where a false match only inflated one skill's list, here it distorts the AGGREGATE 2×2; documented in `suggest-criteria-adjustments.ts` (mirroring suggest-upgrades' inherited-residual comment, explicitly noting the aggregate distortion and that `matchedPostings` is the human's only spot-check); a stopword list is a recorded future item, not this story. **GATES (bare, per slice):** `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean; root `pnpm test` **1384 passing** (up from 1342: +22 core/scoring, +6 db, +10 api, +4 web) *(2026-07-26, M4-04 — living-ledger correction, prior text retained: "1380" identified as the stale figure by the M4-03 arbiter (BACKLOG record below), which recorded the live bare `pnpm test` on a zero-new-tests tree = 1384 passing / 135 files; the record's own component sum 1342+22+6+10+4 = 1384 agrees. ### M4-03 · Platform deployment ADR · **S** · `done`

- ADR-0015 written: decide platform hosting (stay local / Azure / PaaS) with cost and career rationale; implement only if the decision is trivial to execute inside the week. *(2026-07-19: renumbered from ADR-0008 — that number was taken by M2-01's portfolio-hosting ADR, created first. Answers, from six pre-registered legs (full evidence in the M10-01 BUILD RECORD under the M10 section): (1) `runtimeConfig.public.apiBase` lands ONLY in the Nitro server bundle (`.output/server/chunks/nitro/nitro.mjs`), NEVER in `.output/public/**`, which carries NO entry HTML at all (only `_nuxt/` assets) - `nuxt build` ssr:false is a Nitro-server-served SPA, not a static bundle. (2) Serve-time `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE` DOES inject - the Nitro server writes it into `window.__NUXT__.config` per request (confirmed at byte + client-boot levels). (3) A pure static serve of `.output/public/` carries whatever the build baked, which is neither an entry document nor config. (4) BUILD-time env is INERT - a sentinel value AND the empty string both leave the default `localhost:4301`; same-origin is NOT reachable via build-time env, only via serve-time runtime env (`''` -> relative URLs). (5) Consequence: the AWS demo (M10-02) should serve the SPA via its Nitro node server with `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE` set at container runtime, NOT via `@fastify/static` of build output; the plan-§78 finding was routed to plan-m10 + review-seat to reshape the m10-02 draft. **E2E RIDER DISPOSITION:** build+preview IS now demonstrably provable (`nuxt build` then `node .output/server/index.mjs` serves the working app - used as this probe's own Leg B/F harness), but moving the e2e web server off `nuxt dev` is e2e strategy, NOT M10 work - PARKED as a named future story **E2E-BUILD-PREVIEW** (owner: a future test-infra story), deliberately NOT implemented here.)*

## 12-week retro (week 12, 2026-07-26)

- **Roadmap COMPLETE.** All five milestones shipped — M0 Foundation, M1 Job Intelligence MVP, M2 Portfolio MVP, M3 Skill Accelerator, M4 Integrations & Hardening. Every story reached `main` by branch + PR + green checks + CAS merge. **70 PRs merged through M4-03** (the highest PR number is #71 — GitHub shares numbering with issues, so #71 is NOT a PR count); this closing story adds its feature PR + the terminal close-record PR. **18 forward-only migrations** (0000–0017, drizzle-generated, checked in). **15 ADRs** (`docs/DECISIONS/` 0001–0015, gapless — verified firsthand). **OpenAPI at 45 paths** (`jq '.paths | length' docs/api/openapi.json`). Bare `pnpm test` on this docs-only branch (adds no tests) = **1384 passing / 135 files** — the number this branch's own gate printed (see the M4-04 build record).

- **The three tracks (PLAN §6):**
  - **Evidence-track — MET.** Public TypeScript monorepo (architecture, 15 ADRs, tests, CI, security thinking a hiring manager can actually read) + the portfolio live at carlosgutz.com with **seven** honest case studies (the ≥6 criterion was MET at M2-07), each provenance-labeled (professional / personal / personal-AI-assisted) with results sourced to a resolvable citation, enforced by a deterministic build-time gate (ADR-0010).
  - **Hired-track — attested at the M1 gate; current status operator-owned.** Applications ran manually from week 1 (never gated on the tool). At the M1-13 dogfood gate the tool scored 5 real postings end to end and its fit reports informed 6 of 6 in-flight applications (mode prep ×6), per the M1 retro's firsthand + attested record. Current application outcomes are operator-owned and not re-gathered here (no company names, no application specifics — the M1-retro privacy pattern).
  - **Skill-track — machinery shipped; the discrete dogfood deliverable is NOT evidenced in this ledger (honest finding).** The full accelerator shipped and is tested: learning plans (M3-01), exercises linked to gaps (M3-02), mastery evidence (M3-03), interview-prep packs (M3-04), revisit scheduling (M3-05), and evidence → `profile_skills.level` upgrades (M3-06). But PLAN §6 criterion 3 ("at least 2 genuine gaps from real postings converted into completed, evidence-backed exercises") — and the PLAN week-11 line "Dogfood on Carlos's top real gaps" (PLAN:115) — has **no completion record in this delivery ledger** (the only "dogfood" entries are the M1-13 gate and M3-01's LLM live-smoke legs, neither of which is a gap conversion). Recorded honestly, never fabricated: the machinery to satisfy the criterion is built and tested, but claiming it MET requires operator attestation that this ledger does not carry. Dispositioned as an environment finding in the M4-04 build record.

- **The flywheel — three edges BUILT** (ARCHITECTURE:397, "the flywheel in data"): application outcomes → removal-only `search_criteria` adjustments (M4-02, the first edge closed), completed exercises → case-study drafts (M4-01), mastery evidence → `profile_skills.level` upgrades (M3-06). All deterministic, human-confirmed, audited; no LLM in any edge.

- **Platform posture:** local-first by decision (ADR-0015 — the platform holds real private career data; the privacy rationale was decisive, not cost), portfolio publicly deployed secretless via GitHub Pages (ADR-0008). No platform was ever hosted; no platform secret was ever created.

- **LLM engineering inventory (strong employability signal, all ledger-derivable):** five production prompt families in a **sha256-pinned versioned registry** — extract-requirements@v1, improvement-plan@v1, learning-plan@v1, interview-prep@v1, and resume-tailoring versioned **v1 → v2** (M2-12) — under the new-behavior-equals-new-version law (a shipped prompt is never edited in place). All job-posting text is untrusted input (delimited data with a per-request random boundary token, never interpolated into system prompts — ADR-0006); the **injection corpus (13 fixtures, ADR-0006 Accepted)** and the drafting adversarial corpus (4 fixtures) both live-passed against the real provider, and every story's adversarial/live legs passed. Deterministic scoring is walled from the LLM (`packages/scoring` never imports `packages/llm`); the LLM extracts and drafts, never scores, never sends. Total recorded live-provider spend across all legs was on the order of tens of cents (per-story figures in the ledger; e.g. M3-04 ≈ $0.14).

## v2 backlog (recorded 2026-07-26, M4-04)

The 12-week roadmap is v1. These are the recorded v2 candidates; each names its prerequisite gate. **Newly named candidates:**

- **Platform deployment (LEAD).** Decided deferred by ADR-0015 (stay local-first). Reopens on any of ADR-0015's four triggers — multi-user, a remote-access need, an example-data public demo instance, or a deliberate deploy decision; any deploy is its own ADR + a STOP-and-ask on the first platform secret. **Carries the M1-02 e2e park** as a prerequisite question (whether NUXT_PUBLIC_* runtime overrides inject into a prebuilt ssr:false payload — and whether to move the e2e web server to build+preview if so). Carlos ratified (2026-07-26) an **append-only ADR-0015 amendment** noting an Azure Container Apps consumption-tier path (~$12–15/mo) as a cheaper Azure entry point that does NOT change the local-first decision (privacy was decisive, not cost).
- **Automated job collection with legal guardrails.** Prerequisites are the RISKS L-01 invariants (respect ToS / robots.txt / rate limits / auth boundaries; never bypass CAPTCHAs or anti-bot protections) plus its own ADR before any collection code. Today the MVP ingests pasted text only.
- **Multi-user support.** ADR-0015 trigger 1; the schema already carries `user_id` everywhere, so this is possible without a redesign.
- **Email/calendar integration.** From the original roadmap (M4 out-of-scope, v2); closes application tracking into the operator's calendar/inbox.
- **Resume tailoring/export — honest status: v1 SHIPPED.** The M4-04 AC listed this as a v2 candidate, but v1 already shipped (M2-10 spec-not-prose tailoring + M2-12 experience-bullet selection, promoted from the Icebox at the M1 retro Q5; ADR-0012). The remaining v2 slice is **export beyond ADR-0012's markdown-first, manual framing** (document-format export) — stated against the ADR's own words, not a fabricated format list.

**Standing candidates already recorded in Parked / Icebox (pointers, not duplicated text):**

- **Scoring v2** — Icebox C3+C4+C7 (min_quals denominator; category-aware gap handling; the false-positive hard-filter exclusion class). The Icebox entry says it "feeds M4-04's v2 backlog" — this pointer discharges that.

- **Exclusion-override / reclassification UX** — Icebox C8 (no in-product correction path for a false exclusion).
- **Cross-plan gap → exercises read** — Icebox (a gap-first view of skill work across every plan).

*(2026-07-27 BUILD RECORD, one PR, single `feat(M6-04)` commit.)* Fourth A1 (Resume, Track A) v2 build story - the FLAGSHIP composed-with-provenance wiring. **MIGRATION + a new route-level enforcement site => external-review class (a)**, and the gate-update ledger's **route-level tamper proof ships its demonstrated detection** in the same change. **NO new ADR** (ADR-0018 names M6-04 service/routes/tables as its wiring consumer; the M3-04/M7-02 no-new-ADR precedent). **NO new prompt version** (`resume-compose@v1` consumed frozen, so NO live-pass owed - the per-version live pass was M6-03's). **Executed from `plans/m6-04.approved.md`** (autonomous dual gate: review-seat verdict:PASS after one fold round + ceremony co-approval, REVIEW-QUEUE hash pin `efb516247be59e97`; Carlos's per-plan word WAIVED per PROTOCOL "plan approvals join the autonomous window"; approved.md byte-identical to the pin). **Work:** (1) **Migration 0019** (`resume_compose_runs` audit, `resume_documents` pinned artifact, `resume_claims`, `resume_claim_citations` provenance ledger), drizzle-kit-generated, **additive/forward-only, no hand-edits** - 4 CREATE TABLE. **FK durability law (the resume_variants recipe):** every PROFILE-row FK is **ON DELETE SET NULL** (navigation; the M0-08 full-sync re-import must not mutate a reviewed artifact), the OWNERSHIP spine (user/report/run/document/claim) is CASCADE; the `resume_claims` section<->entity CHECK is **implication-form** and the `resume_claim_citations` source CHECK is **at-most-one-non-null** (both SET-NULL-tolerant - a tombstoned pointer never violates them; INSERT-time exactly-one is a service invariant), plus the **partial `resume_documents_current_unique WHERE superseded_at IS NULL`** (the M3-06 active-row precedent = "one current document per report", the compose cache). (2) **`packages/core`** (additive) - `RESUME_COMPOSE_RUN_STATUSES` (5 wire + the `flagged`/`empty` policy statuses), `RESUME_DOCUMENT_REVIEW_STATUSES`, `CITATION_SOURCE_KINDS`, `profileContactLink(s)Schema`, `canonicalResumeDocSchema`, and the `{run, document, cached}` response schemas. (3) **`packages/db`** - `resume-documents.repository.ts`: `getComposeInputs` (the REQUIRED-1 server-derived read), the single-transaction `persistComposeOutcome`, and the cache/redraft-CAS/review-CAS reads. (4) **`packages/llm`** - added the compose builder/prompt exports to the package index (the M6-03 consumption seam). (5) **`apps/api/src/modules/resume-compose/`** - the service (DB reads -> `buildComposePayload` -> `runPrompt(resumeComposeV1)` -> `checkClaimProvenance` -> the single policy site -> persist) + 4 routes (POST/GET `/fit-reports/:id/resume-document`, POST `/resume-documents/:id/{redraft,review}`) + DI in `app.ts`; **OpenAPI regen 45 -> 48 paths**. **THE FIVE FORWARD OBLIGATIONS, each discharged with a test:** (1) **links-jsonb zod** at the read boundary (`profileContactLinksSchema.safeParse`; a malformed row -> `MalformedContactLinksError`). (2) **route-level tamper proof** - the single policy site `deriveComposeRunStatus` + the one-tx `persistComposeOutcome` write the document + claims + citation ledger ONLY on an ok, gate-passing, non-empty run; a fabricated-number (L2) OR cross-provenance (L4) mocked claim lands the run `flagged` and writes NOTHING; **DEMONSTRATED DETECTION (firsthand):** neutering `deriveComposeRunStatus` to return the wire status turned EXACTLY the fabricated-number, cross-provenance, and empty-policy tests red (3 failed | 8 passed), restored -> 11/11 green (`claim-provenance.ts` byte-untouched). (3) **empty-draft policy** - a zero-claim ok draft is vacuously gate-ok but records status `empty` and persists NO document (distinct from `flagged`; both pinned). (4) **REQUIRED-1 server-derived inputs** - the route reads NO client gate input (evidence/entities/vocabulary/claims all server-originated); a doctored request body has zero effect (test). (5) **compile-time assignability pin** - the service passes the builder sent-set into `checkClaimProvenance` with NO cast (the live pin), plus explicit bidirectional `AssertAssignable` assertions in `resume-compose.provenance-pin.test.ts` (any drift between the llm mirror and the scoring input fails `pnpm typecheck`). **Plan folds (all three audit REQUIRED + advisories):** FK-durability SET-NULL/implication/at-most-one (REQUIRED-1); the concurrent-compose race recovery (REQUIRED-2) - `persistComposeOutcome` uses an **untargeted `onConflictDoNothing()`** so BOTH interleavings are swallowed into a 200 cache-hit (the same-revision collision AND the TOCTOU leg where a compose computed rev N+1 while a concurrent one made rev N current -> the partial current-unique; the rev-N+1 leg the plan's revision-target arbiter would have 500'd was caught during execution); education + report gaps added to the compose read + the derived-stale set (REQUIRED-3); the review CAS guards `superseded_at IS NULL` (ADVISORY-A); missing contact -> 409 profile-incomplete (ADVISORY-B); the derived `stale` flag's deletion-blindness recorded (ADVISORY-C). **DEVIATIONS (disclosed):** (a) mastery_evidence carries no user-authored prose (kind/url/date, and its exercise prompt is LLM-generated), so it is NOT a compose citation source in M6-04 - the builder is passed empty mastery evidence; the `mastery_evidence` citation FK + `CITATION_SOURCE_KINDS` entry are retained for a future prose-bearing story. (b) the non-persisting outcome is modelled as the house `{run, document:null, cached}` shape with `run.status` (`flagged`/`empty`) the discriminant, rather than the plan's sketch `{composed:false, status}` envelope - a more idiomatic realization of the same semantics (the resume_variant twin). (c) the concurrent-race arbiter is untargeted rather than the plan's `(fit_report_id, revision)` target, to cover the TOCTOU leg (above). (d) the Write-tool raw-NUL hazard struck ONCE (`resume-document.ts` NUL-check literal), caught pre-commit by the per-file scan and rewritten via `String.fromCharCode(0)` (printable-ASCII source). **GATES (bare, never piped, `TEST_DB_SUFFIX=_a1`, node 24 per `.nvmrc`):** `pnpm typecheck` **0** · `pnpm lint` **0** (prettier reflowed the new files + the drizzle 0019 meta - applied, re-checked bare; `db:generate` confirms no schema drift) · `pnpm test` **0** = **162 files / 1739 tests** (firsthand baseline **158 files / 1703 tests** on branch base `455fe8b`; **+4 files / +36 tests** = 14 core wire + 10 db repository + 11 api route + 1 assignability pin, strictly additive; the M1-07 extraction corpus + sibling adversarial corpora untouched and green; the openapi-drift test green after regen incl. its `rawText`-in-exactly-two-locations guard). **ASCII-only new source**; per-artifact NUL/C0 scan CLEAN on every committed blob; privacy-check content leg PASS (temp-copy recipe). - **M6-05** · `packages/resume-render` · **L** · `done` — PDF/DOCX/markdown/plaintext/JSON from the canonical doc; determinism spike + golden bytes; parse audit (pdf-parse/mammoth round-trip); export routes (`?format=`) streaming, reviewed-only.

*(2026-07-28 BUILD RECORD, one PR, single `feat(M6-05)` commit.)* Fifth A1 (Resume, Track A) v2 build story - the render + export leg over M6-04's canonical snapshot. **TWO new verification gates (determinism goldens + parse-audit) + a new eslint module-wall block => external-review class (a)**, so each gate change ships a **demonstrated planted-FAIL** in the same change (THREE total). **NO new ADR** (ADR-0018 sec 124-131 names the libraries + determinism + reviewed-only + parse-audit + the module wall as its already-recorded export posture; the M6-04/M3-04 no-new-ADR precedent) - only an **append-only ADR-0018 "M6-05 determinism outcome" amendment** (D12), because the spike landed PDF+DOCX content-golden rather than byte-golden, so "golden-byte tested" is not left silently overstating. **NO migration, NO schema change, NO new prompt, NO LLM, NO live pass** (pure + deterministic - the `packages/scoring` class; CI proves it entirely). **Executed from `plans/m6-05.approved.md`** (autonomous dual gate: review-seat verdict:PASS + ceremony co-approval, REVIEW-QUEUE hash pin `ade767885faeec5f`; Carlos's per-plan word WAIVED per PROTOCOL "plan approvals join the autonomous window"; approved.md byte-identical to the pin). **Work:** (1) **NEW pure workspace `packages/resume-render`** (`@careerforge/resume-render`, raw-TS no-build) importing ONLY `@careerforge/core` across the workspace boundary (external npm libs pdfmake/docx/pdf-parse/mammoth are permitted). **Two-layer module wall:** D1a manifest-pin test asserts the `@careerforge/*` SUBSET of `dependencies` == `['@careerforge/core']` (NOT scoring's all-keys assertion, since the external libs are legitimate deps) + `INTERNAL_DEPENDENCIES` built from the real core import; D1b a new eslint block `{ files: ['packages/resume-render/**'], rules: restrict(SQL, LLM_SDK, INTERNAL_EXCEPT_CORE) }` RE-LISTING the full restriction set (no-restricted-imports is last-block-wins). (2) **Five renderers** over `CanonicalResumeDoc` from a shared `layout.ts` (identical single-column ATS-safe structure across formats): markdown/plaintext/json pure strings, PDF via pdfmake (embedded OFL font), DOCX via `docx`. `renderResume(format, doc)` is **ASYNC** (`Promise<RenderedResume>`; pdfmake/docx buffer production is Promise-based). **Untrusted-text rendering law (ADR-0018 sec 70):** dynamic strings are literal text in every format - markdown backslash-escapes the metacharacter set (a hostile-claim non-interpretation test pins it), pdf/docx place plain runs. (3) **OFL font** - IBM Plex Sans (OFL-1.1) 4 styles (`.ttf`) + `OFL.txt`, embedded in the PDF vfs deterministically; a repo-root `.gitattributes` declares `*.ttf`/`*.otf` `binary` (composes with the pre-existing `apps/portfolio/.gitattributes` `*.woff2 binary`). (4) **Determinism (D4), SPIKE FIRST:** the spike rendered PDF+DOCX twice, diffed, and DECIDED per format - **md/txt/json BYTE-golden**; **PDF + DOCX CONTENT-golden** (pdf-parse/mammoth extracted-text snapshots): pdfmake emits no live `/CreationDate` and its random trailer `/ID` is pinned length-preserving, but fontkit's font-subset tables order by V8's per-process hash seed (byte-stable within a process, divergent across processes - not reproducible in CI), and the docx zip carries non-pinnable per-entry wobble. Double-render equality (byte for md/txt/json, extracted-text for pdf/docx) + committed goldens. (5) **Parse-audit (D6)** - `auditParse(format, bytes, doc)` (pdf-parse/mammoth round-trip) computes TWO SEPARATE, NEVER-MERGED results (`parseIntegrity` structural anchors in order, reporting LABELS only never values; `evidenceIntegrity` every claim text verbatim, reporting POSITIONS only) + a fixed render-fidelity honesty string ("not a prediction of any real ATS"). (6) **`packages/core`** (additive) - `resumeExportFormatSchema` (5), `resumeAuditFormatSchema` (pdf/docx), `parseAuditReportSchema`. (7) **`packages/db`** - one additive lean `getDocumentById(userId, documentId)` (row only, userId-scoped). (8) **`apps/api/src/modules/resume-compose/`** (additive) - `resume-export.service.ts` + `resume-compose.export.routes.ts` (2 GET routes) + DI in `app.ts` + `@careerforge/resume-render` dep; **OpenAPI regen 48 -> 50 paths**. **`GET /resume-documents/:id/export?format=`** streams the rendered file (identity-serializer raw body, correct Content-Type + Content-Disposition ASCII slug filename, NO server file write - **P-01 zero file writes**), **reviewed + non-superseded only re-derived server-side** (409 draft `DOCUMENT_NOT_EXPORTABLE` vs 409 superseded `DOCUMENT_SUPERSEDED`, distinct codes), 404 not-found/not-owned, 500 malformed-snapshot (zod safeParse at the jsonb read boundary), the 200 has NO response schema so it is absent from OpenAPI by omission (no binary-response spec problem). **`GET /resume-documents/:id/parse-audit?format=pdf|docx`** returns the JSON report (superseded-gated but DRAFT-ALLOWED - a diagnostic). **Never-trust-the-client (D9):** the only inputs are `:id` + `?format` + `request.user`; a doctored request body has zero effect (test). **Value-free logs (D10):** ids/format/byte-length/booleans only. **THREE DEMONSTRATED PLANTED-FAILS (each red-captured-then-restored):** **D-GATE-1** determinism - mutating a fixture claim (`twelve`->`TWENTY`) turned the md byte-golden AND the pdf/docx content-goldens red (2 failed), restored -> 4/4 green. **D-GATE-2** lint wall - a forbidden `import '@careerforge/db'` in `render.ts` turned BARE `pnpm lint` red (`no-restricted-imports`, the M6-05 wall message), removed -> green. **D-GATE-3** parse-audit - neutering `evidenceIntegrity` to always `{ ok: true }` turned the tamper-detection tests red (3 failed - a claim-removed artifact slips through), restored -> 6/6 green. **DEVIATIONS (disclosed):** (a) `renderResume` + `auditParse` are ASYNC (the plan's ADVISORY-A for render; auditParse is async because pdf-parse/mammoth are Promise-based - a necessary realization, not a scope change). (b) PDF landed **content-golden, not byte-golden** (the spike outcome; R2 anticipated) - so the plan's REQUIRED-1 path-scoped `.gitattributes` binary entry for a byte-golden `.pdf`/`.docx` fixture is UNNEEDED (no byte-golden binary fixture exists; all goldens are plain-ASCII TEXT and scanned normally); only the embedded fonts are declared binary. UI-follows-merged-API for the M6-04 compose endpoints (`POST`/`GET /fit-reports/:id/resume-document`, `POST /resume-documents/:id/redraft`, `POST /resume-documents/:id/review`) + the M6-05 render/export endpoints (`GET /resume-documents/:id/export?format=`, `GET /resume-documents/:id/parse-audit?format=`), all on main since PR#99/#109. Adds six typed client methods to `use-api.ts` (`getResumeDocument` / `composeResumeDocument` / `redraftResumeDocument` / `reviewResumeDocument` / `getResumeParseAudit` / `exportResumeDocument`, type-only core imports; export is the raw-fetch->Blob->anchor download helper with a per-format extension map, same shape as `exportResumeVariant`/`exportCaseStudy`) and one component — **`components/ResumeStudioSection.vue`**, mounted in the **Prepare** tab of `pages/postings/[id].vue` **above** `ResumeVariantSection` so the composed doc is PRIMARY and the M2-10 tailoring variant is clearly SECONDARY (the ADR-0018 "never present one as the other" law). **Behavior:** compose is **review-gated** (`report.reviewStatus === 'reviewed'` — the tailoring precedent; a draft report shows `rs-review-gate`) and a fire-once paid LLM call (10-20 s pending); a `flagged` gate violation or an `empty` zero-claim draft persists nothing (`document: null`, `run.status` the discriminant) and shows a loud `role="alert"` `rs-failed-run` banner that does NOT hide the compose button (re-POST is the retry). Because `GET` always returns `run: null`, the flagged/empty terminal and run telemetry are captured from the compose/redraft **POST response** into a local `lastOutcome` ref, and the GET is refreshed only when a document actually persisted. A composed document renders the deterministic **contact / education / skills** facts plus the gated **claims grouped by section** (`summary`/`experience`/`project` in core order), each with its resolved `entityLabel` and a citation `<details>` fold (source-kind label + durable `sourceText`); a **stale** chip when `document.stale`; a draft/reviewed `AppStateChip`. Draft → one-shot **Mark reviewed** (optional notes, empty sends `null`); a reviewed doc offers **Export** (a `<select>` over the five `RESUME_EXPORT_FORMATS` + button) and a compact **render-fidelity parse-audit** (a `RESUME_AUDIT_FORMATS` pdf/docx select → the two never-merged integrity results as reviewed/danger chips + missing-anchor/out-of-order/missing-claim-position detail + the honesty string); **Redraft** (supersede + draft N+1, a fresh paid call) is available on any current document. Run telemetry (model/prompt/tokens/latency/status) shows in a footer after a compose/redraft. **RENDERING LAW (M1-02):** claim `text` and citation `sourceText` are user-composed/verified prose but UNTRUSTED on display — `{{ }}`-only interpolation; contact `links` render as escaped TEXT (label + url), NEVER an `<a href>` (S-02, the artifactUrl precedent). The section/skill-level/citation-kind/run-status/export-format display vocab are LOCAL typed `Record<Enum, …>` maps + typed lists (the M1-11 vite-optimizer law keeps core's zod out of the bundle — the ResumeVariantSection/skills-page precedent), pinned complete against core's `RESUME_CLAIM_SECTIONS` / `SKILL_LEVELS` / `CITATION_SOURCE_KINDS` / `RESUME_COMPOSE_RUN_STATUSES` / `RESUME_EXPORT_FORMATS` / `RESUME_AUDIT_FORMATS` by test. **CONTRACTS HELD:** no existing `data-testid` churned (all-new `rs-*` testids); `e2e/postings-xss.spec.ts` merges **byte-unchanged** (the Prepare-tab addition keeps every pinned name; the spec diff vs main is empty and the e2e re-run passes); `@careerforge/core` type-only import law holds; **no raw hex** (M8-08 ratchet green — tokens only, mono via `--font-mono`). New testids covered by the NEW `tests/resume-studio-section.test.ts` (13 tests: compose review-gate, compose-once + refetch renders the doc, full document render (contact/claims-grouped/education/skills), citation fold, stale chip, draft shows review-form-not-export + review refetches, reviewed shows export-form-not-review + exports the chosen format, parse-audit renders integrity chips + missing detail + honesty, redraft posts the current id + refetches, flagged compose persists nothing + loud banner + keeps button + no refetch, empty terminal, vocab complete vs all six core enums, hostile claim/citation/contact/entity text inert). **GATES (bare, `set -o pipefail`, node 24 per `.nvmrc`, `TEST_DB_SUFFIX=_b2web`):** `pnpm typecheck` **0** (all projects) · `pnpm lint` **0** (prettier reflowed the two new files — applied, re-checked bare) · `pnpm test` **0** = **1903 passed / 177 files** (dockerized Postgres up for the integration legs); `postings-xss` e2e PASS (exit 0, isolated 4320/4321); per-artifact NUL/C0 scan clean on the committed blobs (literal-full-SHA, PIPE `git show`, bytes>0) + new `.ts` test pure printable ASCII (**four em-dashes slipped into the NEW `use-api.ts` comments — caught by the source-byte grep before commit, fixed to hyphens; the pre-existing `use-api.ts` em-dashes are the grandfathered existing-file style, unchanged; the `.vue` template typography stays**); `node scripts/privacy-check.mjs` via the sanctioned temp-copy method (docs touched). Adds four typed client methods to `use-api.ts` (`getGameplan` / `draftGameplan` / `reviewGameplan` / `toggleGameplanCheck`, type-only core imports) and one component — **`components/GameplanSection.vue`**, mounted in the **Prepare** tab of `pages/postings/[id].vue` BELOW `InterviewPrepSection` as the culminating pursuit-strategy surface. **Behavior:** the ADR-0019 "three views" realized as a scoped `role=tablist` with roving tabindex — **Apply** (the `strategySummary` + the four phase strategies in GAMEPLAN_PHASES order), **Speak** (the 0..6 STAR stories, each a requirement line + S/T/A/R + a citations fold carrying the direct/partial/adjacent strength and the posting/profile quotes), **Process** (the deterministic checklist grouped by phase + the application-stage timeline). Drafting is **review-gated** (`report.reviewStatus === 'reviewed'`; a draft report shows `gp-review-gate`) and a fire-once paid LLM call (10-20 s pending); a `flagged` tripwire terminal (message-likeness / citation-provenance / external-pointer) or any non-ok run persists nothing, and the GET run-selection contract surfaces the latest failure run into a loud `role="alert"` `gp-failed-run` banner that does NOT hide the draft button (re-POST is the retry). No redraft, no export (cache-once, ADR-0019 consequence B — regeneration is a re-score; a gameplan is coaching, never a document). The **checklist toggle** is the user's OWN process state (allowed pre-review, D6): `POST /checks` returns the FULL overlay and the UI takes it as authoritative (never computes `done` client-side), one toggle at a time with a `gp-check-error` alert that never drops the item. The **applications link card** (Process view) links the stage timeline to the posting's tracked application (`/applications/:id`) when one exists, else a "start tracking" note — closing the gameplan/tracker loop (M1-03), via an optional `trackedApplicationId` prop the parent feeds from its `trackedApplication` probe. Meta-only **sibling pointers** (improvement plan / interview prep, id+status only) render in the lead. ### M8-03 · Fraunces self-hosting · **M** · `done`

*(2026-07-27 BUILD RECORD, one PR, `feat(M8-03)` — status `done` on build-complete + all LOCAL gates green; the CI 7/7 + final CI Lighthouse numbers + the term-5 ramp evaluation + merge/SEAL line are recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **Third B1 (portfolio/design lane) v2 story; discharges ADR-0016 §3 (font self-hosting policy) — NO new ADR.** **CLASS (a) executable** (adds `tests/fonts.test.ts`, edits `nuxt.config.ts`, and commits the repo's FIRST binary asset) — external pre-merge glance REQUIRED. It MODIFIES no existing verification gate, so no planted-FAIL is strictly owed; the new wiring gate's assertions were nonetheless demonstrated-to-fail per lane discipline (below). Executed from `plans/m8-03.approved.md` (sha256 `4792e1d4…`), dual-approved (review-seat PASS + ceremony co-approval).

**What landed — the self-hosted Fraunces display face in `apps/portfolio`:** (1) `public/fonts/Fraunces-latin-var.woff2` (34308 bytes, sha256 `db77a660…`; the RECIPE.md instanced+latin-subset Modified Version — wght variable 100..900, opsz pinned 144, SOFT/WONK dropped); (2) `public/fonts/OFL.txt` (SIL OFL-1.1 text + copyright, shipped alongside per the license — **RFN finding: the copyright line declares NO Reserved Font Name**, so retaining the `Fraunces` @font-face family name for the Modified Version is compliant); (3) `.gitattributes` `*.woff2 binary` (repo's FIRST binary asset); (4) `app/assets/css/fonts.css` — a `@font-face` for Fraunces (`font-weight: 100 900`, `font-display: optional` [ramp, below]) + a metric-adjusted `'Fraunces Fallback'` @font-face, with a full provenance comment; (5) `nuxt.config.ts` — `fonts.css` in the `css` array + a `<link rel=preload as=font type=font/woff2 … crossorigin>`; (6) `base.css` — a `--font-display` var (in base.css, NOT tokens.css — D6 keeps the color ratchet clean) + `:is(h1,h2,h3){ font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 600 }` (explicit weight, never unset → avoids the 900-black hazard; minimal h1–h3 application makes the preload honest, broad restyle is M8-04); (7) `CLAUDE.md` — the binary-asset source-byte-law carve-out clause (R-2; the one sanctioned edit outside `apps/portfolio`, M6-01 precedent — declared-binary `.gitattributes` paths are exempt from the printable-ASCII/NUL law, text blobs still scanned).

**Metric-adjusted fallback (measured firsthand, fonttools 4.60.2; recomputed by three seats to an exact match):** FRAUNCES upem 2000 / xAvg 1088 / ascent 1956 / descent -510 / lineGap 0 / USE_TYPO_METRICS=1; GEORGIA (donor) upem 2048 / xAvg 901 / ascent 1878 / descent -449 / lineGap 0. Per-em-normalized (no dropped-upem bug): **size-adjust 123.65%, ascent-override 79.09%, descent-override 20.62%, line-gap-override 0%** (box math closes: 0.7909 × 1.2365 = 0.978 em = Fraunces typo ascent). **DISCLOSED PLAN DEVIATION:** size-adjust 123.65% is OUTSIDE the approved plan's heuristic 90–115% band — but NOT the dropped-upem bug the band targets (unnormalized ratio 120.75%, a ~3-point signature not ~2×); the cause is physical (the opsz=144 display cut is ~24% wider per-em than Georgia, and no universally-available wider system serif exists as donor). Accepted "as measured, with disclosure" by **dual concurrence** (review-seat + ceremony, 2026-07-27); the three independent recomputes are a stronger guard than the band.

**Demonstrated-FAIL (new gate assertions, per lane discipline; each transient, restored, never committed red):** (1) removed the preload `crossorigin` → `fonts.test.ts` RED on the crossorigin assertion (1 failed | 6 passed); (2) `font-weight: 100 900` → `400` → RED on the weight-range assertion. Both restored → 7/7 green.

Never committed red (temp-dir + `.lighthouseci` gitignored). **REVIEW ADVISORIES folded (both non-blocking, no re-audit):** (A) `.lighthouseci/` is gitignored by the ROOT `.gitignore:35`, NOT a nonexistent `apps/portfolio/.gitignore` - S4 check corrected + verified (`git check-ignore` -> IGNORED). ## M9 — Skill Signal + Dogfood (v2, week 7)

- **M9-01** · Operator dogfood (v1 skill criterion) · **operator** · `planned` — 2 genuine-gap exercises completed with evidence, a case-study draft generated, a skill-upgrade confirmed/rejected; friction logged (Claude assists, does not fabricate the attestation). **Gates milestone closure, not story starts.**

*(FINDINGS APPEND (2026-07-29, operator-run platform dogfood, second round — the skill criterion above stays open; this append records the findings pass): operator dogfooding against a real posting (details private; a wholly fictionalized regression fixture is owed in the correctness arc) plus an external repo review surfaced one architectural defect class and one documentation defect class. Every claim was verified firsthand against main `fe06a2a` before recording — via `git show origin/main:` blob reads, never a stale tree — and two external claims were REFUTED and are recorded so they are not re-litigated. CONFIRMED (→ v2.1 correctness arc, plan in the ops directory awaiting the dual-approval flow): (1) `classify-gaps.ts` treats absence of named-skill evidence as `genuine_gap` — the default fall-through; the taxonomy has no unknown/insufficient-evidence class and cannot distinguish "no evidence found" from "evidence confirms unmet". (2) The classifier is category-blind: seniority/comp/location/administrative requirements ladder through skill evidence (extends the category-aware v2 candidate parked in the M1-11 close-out's friction-signal append). (3) The seniority dimension's deterministic years comparison never propagates to gap classification and emits only `adjacent`-strength evidence — one requirement can simultaneously score as satisfied and classify as `genuine_gap` (the arc's motivating cross-system inconsistency). (4) No durable-profile-facts concept exists (e.g. a work-authorization class), so administrative boilerplate requirements surface as gaps and generate coaching noise. (5) Documentation trailed implementation (OpenAPI path count, fresh-install steps, bootstrap reset guidance, PLAN status) — DISCHARGED by PR #123 → main `4982f1d`. PARKED with named stories (deliberately OUT of the correctness arc so the fixes stay attributable): atomic AND/OR/one-of requirement extraction + within-requirement evidence cross-attachment; skill-model split (proficiency/recency/evidence-strength as separate dimensions — reopens M1-11's D9) EXTENDED per operator direction with research-grounded level semantics, a refresh-cost/time-to-current dimension (a "rusty" skill recoverable in days must not read as absent), and posting-verbiage interpretation (a demanded years figure is a signal to address, never a self-filter); evidence-interview loop (v2.2 resolver for `unknown`, incl. AI-practice evidence records); private-project ingestion (operator-supplied additional private codebases as evidence sources, local-only, same privacy class as `docs/profile/` — never entering the public repo); navigation consolidation + Prepare-as-decision redesign; compose-prompt quality pass; one-button auto-advance pipeline; and the dining-room/kitchen progressive-disclosure principle (decisions and top actions up front, full verboseness one toggle away, nothing removed) recorded as a standing UX principle for the next UI arc. DISMISSED with reasons: "no numeric threshold comparison exists" (false — it exists in the seniority dimension; the defect is propagation, item 3); "the resume engine can only reorder" (conflates the v1 tailoring guide, honestly labeled non-submittable, with resume-compose, which drafts real prose under the claim-provenance gate); "personal projects are ineligible for the composed resume" (false — eligible as project/summary claims; the employment-claim bar is the honesty model working and stays).)*
- **M9-02** · Market-signal aggregation · **M** · `done` — pure `packages/scoring` module + read-only endpoint (per-skill frequency, must-have vs preferred, fit effect, hard-filter presence → Sharpen/Prove/Build/Certify).

*(2026-07-28 BUILD RECORD, one PR, single `feat(M9-02)` commit.)* Seventh A1 (Resume, Track A) v2 build story - a deterministic market-signal aggregator over the user's saved postings' latest fit-report gaps. **NO new ADR** (the M4-02 `suggestCriteriaAdjustments` / M6-06 `scoreAtsCoverage` deterministic-recomputed-read class; the Sharpen/Prove/Build/Certify vocabulary is ratified in V2-PLAN 3.5, the pinned certification-term set follows the M6-06 stopword-set precedent). **NO migration, NO schema change, NO persistence, NO new prompt, NO LLM, NO live pass** (pure + deterministic - the `packages/scoring` class; CI proves it entirely). **Executed from `plans/m9-02.approved.md`** (autonomous dual gate: review-seat verdict:PASS clean round 1 + ceremony co-approval, REVIEW-QUEUE hash pin `db87c1efc41b925f`; approved.md byte-identical to the pin, re-verified at boot). **Work:** (1) **NEW pure aggregator** `packages/scoring/src/market-signal.ts` - `aggregateMarketSignal(instances)` grouping requirement instances by `normalizeWhitespace(requirementText)` (the exact learning-payload recurrence key, a read-only ADR-0006 borrower - paraphrases UNDER-group, the safe direction, R1) and emitting per group: frequency (`postingCount`/`instanceCount`), must-have/preferred split, `excludedPostingCount` (hard-filter presence), the engine's own evidence-weight currency (`bestEvidenceWeight`/`meanEvidenceWeight`), full `classificationCounts` + `overriddenCount`, sorted `categories`, `refs` (the gap.id links), and a certification-mention probe; a pinned first-match ladder (nothing-actionable / all-postings-excluded noAction -> Certify at `CERTIFY_MIN_POSTINGS`=2 distinct non-excluded mentioning postings -> modal actionable classification, ties broken by `BUCKET_SEVERITY`) routes each group into Sharpen/Prove/Build/Certify or noAction. Pure data-in/data-out (double-call + shuffled-input determinism rows; internal id-based `(postingId,fitReportId,gapId)` instance order so caller order can never matter - advisory A1); NO merged/composite score anywhere, `MARKET_SIGNAL_SCORER_VERSION`=1 rides the result. Reuses the fit engine's `normalizeWhitespace`/`tokenizeForMatching` + `round4` + `EVIDENCE_WEIGHTS`; module wall holds (scoring deps stay `['@careerforge/core']`, manifest-pin test green). (2) **BINDING audit-ratified deviation (reviews/PLAN-m9-02.md, review-seat):** D3's `coverageOf(strengths)` will NOT typecheck - exported `coverageOf` takes `EvidenceLink[]` (a 7-field strictObject), while an aggregated instance carries only bare `EvidenceStrength[]` (dragging quote text into the aggregate would be worse). - **M10-01** · M1-02 probe · **S** · `done` — build `apps/web` ssr:false, record where runtimeConfig lands, evidence against BACKLOG:504.
*(2026-08-01 BUILD RECORD, one PR, single `docs(M10-01)` commit - status `done` on probe-run + evidence-recorded + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **Ninth A1 (Resume, Track A) v2 story; the FIRST M10 story - the M1-02 runtime-config probe.** Executed strictly from `plans/m10-01.approved.md` (autonomous dual gate: review-seat PASS + ceremony co-approval, REVIEW-QUEUE:4 pin sha256 first16 `12006b8b7e70a3c8`; the approved file re-verified BYTE-IDENTICAL at boot, full sha256 `12006b8b7e70a3c804fec63fe849ff4148a6b42e86b67a1893079f37a6fde913` == the GO pin). **DOCS-ONLY, CLASS (b) PROSE expected** (the review seat confirms the class) - NO committed probe scripts, NO deps, NO CI/code change; all probe tooling lived in the session scratchpad and died with it. **NO planted-FAIL owed** (no gate created or modified). **Versioned evidence** (the probe is a claim about these exact versions, not eternal): Nuxt **4.4.8**, Nitro **2.13.4**, Vite **7.3.6**, Vue **3.5.39**, Node 24.18.0, pnpm 11.12.0. **The park was answered empirically across six pre-registered legs (each recorded exact command + exit code + observed bytes; no outcome predicted before it ran):** **Leg A (baseline build, no env):** `pnpm --filter @careerforge-app/web build` exit 0 - the default `http://localhost:4301` lands in EXACTLY ONE file, `.output/server/chunks/nitro/nitro.mjs` (as JSON runtimeConfig `"public":{"apiBase":"http://localhost:4301"}`), and in NO `.output/public/**` byte; `.output/public/` carries NO entry HTML (no `index.html`/`200.html`, only `_nuxt/` hashed assets); the client chunk reads `e.public.apiBase` at runtime, never a baked literal. So `nuxt build` (ssr:false) is a **Nitro-server-served SPA, not a static bundle** - the entry HTML is server-rendered per request. **Leg B (serve-time env vs the built Nitro server):** `node .output/server/index.mjs` served `/` carried `apiBase:"http://localhost:4301"` with no env; with `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=http://m10-probe-runtime.invalid:4400` the served HTML's inline `<script>window.__NUXT__.config={public:{apiBase:"..."}}` flipped to the override and the default was GONE - **serve-time env DOES inject** (the park's literal question: YES, in Nitro-serve mode). **Leg C (static-serve, observed bytes):** `@fastify/static` has no server-side templating by construction, so the file it sends is the file on disk; Leg A's inventory IS the evidence - `.output/public/` has no entry HTML and no baked config, so a pure static serve delivers `_nuxt/` assets only, no `/` document, no config (byte-level; no static server stood up per the no-new-deps exclusion). **Leg D (build-time bake, sentinel):** `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=http://m10-probe-build.invalid:4401 pnpm ... build` exit 0 - the sentinel appeared in NEITHER public nor server bytes; the baked default stayed `localhost:4301`. **Build-time env is INERT.** **Leg E (build-time bake, EMPTY STRING - the same-origin candidate):** `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE='' pnpm ... build` exit 0 - nitro.mjs kept `"apiBase":"http://localhost:4301"` and the served HTML kept `apiBase:"http://localhost:4301"`; build-time env is entirely inert for both a value and the empty string. **Leg F (client-boot confirmation, scratchpad Playwright, chromium):** the running client's `window.__NUXT__.config.public.apiBase` (captured at the boot-script assignment via an init-script property trap, before hydration deletes `__NUXT__`) = `"http://localhost:4301"` with no env, `"http://m10-probe-runtime.invalid:4400"` with the override - the CLIENT actually receives the serve-time-injected value ("inject" confirmed at the client-boot level, not merely the byte level). **Serve-time EMPTY STRING (decision-relevant addendum, same serve mechanism):** `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE='' node .output/server/index.mjs` served `apiBase:""` - a RUNTIME empty string DOES inject, yielding relative/same-origin request URLs (`${apiBase}/resume-variants/...` becomes `/resume-variants/...`); same-origin is reachable via the Nitro-serve runtime env, though NOT via build-time env. **THE ANSWER (versioned to the stack above):** `NUXT_PUBLIC_*` runtime overrides DO inject into a prebuilt ssr:false payload WHEN it is served by its own Nitro server - the server writes runtime config into `window.__NUXT__.config` per request from env (Legs B/F); the value lives only in the server bundle, never in `.output/public/**`; BUILD-time env is inert (Legs D/E); a pure static serve of `.output/public/` gets neither an entry document nor config. **CONSEQUENCE for M10-02 (plan §78 escalation - posted to review-seat + plan-m10 INBOXes at 2026-08-01T23:58Z, not buried here):** the m10-02 draft's "`@fastify/static` serves the built payload as static bytes, no Nitro server; same-origin by design" mechanism has NO support in this stack - `.output/public/` has no entry HTML and build-time env cannot bake same-origin. Evidence-backed working paths: **(A)** serve the SPA via its Nitro node server with `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE` set at CONTAINER RUNTIME (serve-time injection works; `''` gives same-origin) - one image, env-configured per environment; **(B)** switch to `nuxt generate` for a truly static `.output/public/index.html`, which needs its OWN build-time-bake probe under `generate` (untested here - generate MAY bake unlike build) + a prod-default apiBase change + loses runtime env configurability. **Recommend (A);** reshaping the m10-02 draft is plan-m10's call (its GO already carries the STOP-on-inert-D/E condition, which this evidence trips). **README:21 left BYTE-UNCHANGED (disclosed executor judgment, Scope IN-2):** the line ("`runtimeConfig.public.apiBase` ... override with `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE`") is a dev-mode statement and the probe CORROBORATES it (the override works at runtime), not contradicts it; the build-time-inert / Nitro-serve nuance is a deployment concern owned by M10-02, not a dev-README correction. **GATES (bare, never piped, node 24 per `.nvmrc`, `TEST_DB_SUFFIX=_a1`):** `pnpm typecheck` **0** · `pnpm lint` **0** (eslint + prettier; the `.claude` bare-lint false-red is fixed on main by PR#117) · `pnpm test` **0** = **205 files / 2306 tests** (docs-only tree; identical to the branch base `bfd8943` - this PR adds no tests). `.output/` is gitignored (verified `git check-ignore` + `git status --porcelain` clean before the docs commit); scratchpad Playwright script + every sentinel build deleted; a final baseline `pnpm ... build` left no sentinel bytes; `git grep` for both `m10-probe-*.invalid` sentinels over the tracked tree is EMPTY (the sentinels appear only in this record's prose as quoted fictional evidence - `.invalid` is an IETF-reserved TLD, zero privacy-gate collision). Per-artifact NUL/C0 scan CLEAN on the committed blob (PIPE `git show`, never capture); this record quotes no control char. `node scripts/privacy-check.mjs` content leg PASS after the final commit, before push (BACKLOG is profile-adjacent docs). **DIFF CONFINED** to `docs/BACKLOG.md` (this record + the M10-01 row flip to `done` + the BACKLOG:504 disposition + the M9-04 SEAL fold below) - README byte-unchanged; no code/schema/prompt/gate/openapi touched. `git diff` confirms) and **NO OpenAPI change** (`docs/api/openapi.json` byte-identical to main). **Four committed slices** on branch `m10-02-containerization` off main `2f2b3371`: **(1) env+host** `4cb7e807` - `WEB_DIST_DIR` (empty-string preprocessed to undefined) + `API_HOST` (default `127.0.0.1`); `main.ts` listens on `env.API_HOST`; `.env.example` documents both names-only; `+2` env tests. **(2a) static mechanism** `f4939171` - `@fastify/static@^10.1.2` wildcard behind `WEB_DIST_DIR`, immutable `/_nuxt/` cache, boot-fail on unreadable entry HTML. **(2b) Accept-aware short-circuit** `008b96da` - the ratified fix for the SPA/API root-path collision; 14 route tests. **(3) container** `fd8f066` (this slice) - Dockerfile, entrypoint, `.dockerignore`, local smoke. **Slice 0 (Leg G probe, scratchpad, no commit) PASSED:** `nuxt generate` (ssr:false) emits a real static entry HTML (`index.html`/`200.html`/`404.html`, no `.output/server`) and BAKES `apiBase:""` same-origin at generate time under `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE=''` - the REV 2 mechanism the M10-01 probe pointed to; no HARD STOP, no nuxt.config fallback needed. **THE r2-A1 AMENDMENT (mid-execution escalation, dual-gate ratified):** the approved D1 had TWO real gaps - (i) "pre-guard registration = public by construction" is FALSE (the global auth hook applies to the static wildcard; the house-idiom fix is stamping `config.public` via `onRoute`), and (ii) a pervasive **SPA/API root-path collision** - `/postings/:id`, `/criteria`, `/skills`, `/applications`, `/case-studies`, `/evidence`, `/learning-plans`, `/review-queue` are guarded API routes the Nuxt SPA ALSO client-routes, so D1's notFound-based SPA fallback and D7's own smoke line for a hard-refreshed deep link were structurally unsatisfiable. I PAUSED (auth-adjacent surface), escalated with a recommended fix, and plan-m10 ratified + review-seat concurred **option (A): an Accept-aware pre-guard `onRequest` short-circuit** - a GET/HEAD request whose `Accept` includes `text/html`, whose final path segment is dot-less, and which is not in `ALWAYS_API_PATHS` (`/health`) is served the SPA shell (200, no-cache); every other request (Accept `application/json`/`*/*`/absent, or a dotted-segment asset path) falls through byte-for-byte to the exact prior API/asset behavior. Both slice-2a fixes were ratified as within-intent corrections; the Fastify sync-hook trap (a 2-arg `onRequest` hook HANGS - the short-circuit hook is `async`) and the `@fastify/static` `setHeaders`-takes-a-`FastifyReply` detail were disclosed. **CONTAINER DESIGN (slice 3, executor's bounded prune choice + one disclosed deviation from STATE's suggested approach):** the STATE-suggested `pnpm --filter @careerforge-app/api deploy` prune is **NOT viable for this repo** - it copies the workspace packages as `.ts` under `node_modules`, and Node 24 refuses to type-strip files under `node_modules` (`ERR_UNSUPPORTED_NODE_MODULES_TYPE_STRIPPING`), which would break `node src/main.ts` at runtime (empirically confirmed in the scratchpad). The runtime image therefore **keeps the physical pnpm workspace layout** (sources run in place from `apps/`/`packages/`, outside `node_modules`, exactly as dev does) and meets the no-nuxt / no-Nitro invariant via a **filtered production install** (`pnpm install --prod --filter "@careerforge-app/api..."`): `apps/web` is not a dependency of `apps/api`, so `nuxt`/`vite`/`vue` never enter the image (build logged "7 of 10 workspace projects"). This also keeps the D5 entrypoint paths **unchanged from the r1 plan** (`node packages/db/src/cli/migrate.ts` then `exec node apps/api/src/main.ts`). Multi-stage `node:24-bookworm-slim` (glibc for `@node-rs/argon2` prebuilts), corepack `pnpm@11.12.0`; web-build stage runs `NUXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE='' nuxt generate`; runtime stage copies only the API's workspace graph + `packages/db/migrations` (incl. `meta/`) + the generated `.output/public` -> `/app/web-dist`; `.output/server` is never built or copied; `USER node`, `ENV API_HOST=0.0.0.0 WEB_DIST_DIR=/app/web-dist`, `EXPOSE 4301`; no secrets, no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (keyless 503 demo posture). `docker-entrypoint.sh` (`set -eu`) migrates then `exec`s the API as PID 1 (committed executable, mode `100755` verified in-diff). `.dockerignore` is default-deny (`*` then `!` allowlist) - `docs/`, `.env*`, `.git`, `.claude/` are structurally outside the build context (context measured at 8.7 MB). **LOCAL SMOKE (`scripts/docker-smoke.mjs`, zero-dep, LOCAL-only - CI has no Docker guarantee, named in the README, closed by the M10-06 runbook):** builds the image, runs it on the compose network against a throwaway scratch DB (`careerforge_docker_smoke`) with throwaway bootstrap creds (random >=24 chars, never printed; Postgres password read from `.env` by the process and passed via name-only `-e` so it never enters argv/`ps`; scratch DB created via the compose container's local-socket `psql` trust so no password crosses the wire), asserts migrate-then-boot (`/health`=200), the same-origin serve (navigation `GET /`=200 text/html with `apiBase:""` baked; `/_nuxt/<asset>`=200 immutable; `/nonexistent` Accept:json=404 JSON envelope; `/postings/123` Accept:text/html=200 shell; `GET /auth/me` unauth=401; throwaway login=200 + session; `/auth/me`+cookie=200) and the image invariants (no `/app/.env`, no `/app/docs`, no nuxt, no `.output/server`, dist carries no `localhost:4301`; plus a web-build-stage assertion that the build context excludes `docs/`/`.env`), teardown always. **16 checks, all green.** **THREE planted-FAILs (D10), each RED->restored, evidence in the PR:** (i) route-pin (slice 2b - neuter the public-surface pin -> allowlist test RED); (iii) Accept predicate (slice 2b - neuter the `text/html` branch -> JSON-404-stays-JSON test RED); (ii) privacy `.dockerignore` (slice 3 - plant a FICTIONAL `docs/profile/PLANTED-FICTIONAL.md` + neuter the docs deny -> the smoke's "build context excludes docs/" assertion RED; restored + plant deleted, 16/16 green). Note the runtime image's `no /app/docs` assertion is a SECOND, independent guard (selective `COPY`), so `.dockerignore`'s protective role is asserted where it acts - the build context - a strengthening over the plan's runtime-image target, disclosed. **DOCUMENTED RESIDUALS:** (1) a JSON/export URL opened directly as a browser navigation (Accept `text/html`) yields the SPA shell rather than the API bytes - the ratified short-circuit's known trade (browsers navigate, API clients send json/`*/*` and stay on the API); (2) a non-navigation `GET /` with Accept `*/*` (no real client) falls through to `@fastify/static`'s directory handling and returns 403 - a benign edge, not the browser path (navigations get the shell). **GATES (bare, never piped, node 24 per `.nvmrc`):** `pnpm typecheck` **0** · `pnpm lint` **0** · `pnpm test` **0** = **206 files / 2322 tests** (the smoke is a standalone script, not a vitest suite, so the count is unchanged from slice 2b). Per-artifact NUL/C0 scan CLEAN on every committed text blob (PIPE `git show`, never capture); all new source is printable ASCII. `node scripts/privacy-check.mjs` content leg run after the final commit, before push (branch is profile-adjacent). region us-east-2) → `aws ecs describe-task-definition` the **server** family → `jq` swaps **only** the image (the two SSM `secrets` are preserved byte-for-byte) and deletes the read-only fields → `register-task-definition` → `update-service` → **`aws ecs wait services-stable`** (the deploy's own failure detector: a boot-failing image goes red, not silently broken). **D4 safety:** `concurrency: deploy-demo` `cancel-in-progress: false`; `timeout-minutes` on both jobs; workflow-level `permissions: {}` (deny-all) with per-job least privilege; a **fork-safety belt** (`github.repository == '<owner>/<repo>'` on both jobs); **NO `pull_request`/`pull_request_target` trigger**. **D5 seam (recorded):** the workflow deploys the **server** task only; the dedicated **seed** task-def is Terraform-owned and not re-rendered here (server-only image changes need no apply; a schema/seed change rides the next `terraform apply` — the binding-amendment seam). **SHA-pin audit (supply-chain law):** every `uses:` is `owner/repo@<full-40-sha>` with a `# vX.Y.Z` comment — `actions/checkout@11bd71901bbe5b1630ceea73d27597364c9af683` (v4.2.2) + `aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@b47578312673ae6fa5b5096b330d9fbac3d116df` (v4.2.1);
