(ii) the workflow's own `image_tag` guard, exercised locally: `latest` and an injection string → exit **1 REJECTED**, `sha-<40hex>` → exit **0 ACCEPTED**. The **LIVE deploy legs** (a role-assume red pre-apply; a deliberately-bad image tag → `services-stable` timeout → red, then a good tag → green, service restored) are **PRE-REGISTERED as OWED into the M10-08 go-live checklist** — they cannot run until the stack exists and the workflow is on `main`. No repo CI workflow-linter exists today (`ci.yml`/`deploy.yml`/`security.yml` only), so `actionlint` ran locally and is recorded here (adding it to CI is deliberately out of scope). **REVIEW ROUND 1 -> 2 (script-injection fix):** R1 CHANGES-REQUIRED found ONE finding — the deploy job (`id-token: write`, the repo's highest-privilege job) interpolated the untrusted `github.event.inputs.image_tag` (and `github.actor`) DIRECTLY into `run` bodies, so a hostile value would execute at expression-substitution time BEFORE the `sha-`regex guard runs — the guard defeated by ordering, and `actionlint` does not flag it (`inputs.*` is outside its untrusted set, reproduced exit 0). R2 fix = GitHub's documented mitigation, applied to EVERY expression-context value that reaches a `run` body (not just the two flagged): each is passed through a step `env:` block and read as a quoted shell variable (`IMAGE_TAG_INPUT`, `GHCR_ACTOR`, `GHCR_TOKEN`, `IMAGE`, `BUILT_IMAGE`, `TASK_DEF_ARN`), so **no `run` body carries `${{ }}`** (audited: zero matches). Re-verified: `actionlint` CLEAN; the injection is now inert — a hostile `image_tag` of `$(touch ...)` flows in as literal DATA, the regex REJECTS it, and nothing executes (demonstrated), while a valid `sha-<40hex>` still passes. **GATES (bare, never piped, node 24 per `.nvmrc`, `TEST_DB_SUFFIX=_a1`; re-run at the R2 head):** `pnpm typecheck` **0** · `pnpm lint` **0** (eslint + prettier; `.github/` is prettier-ignored per repo config, and `prettier --write` on the workflow was a no-op — already prettier-clean) · `pnpm test` **0** = **2373 tests / 217 files** (unchanged — workflow + docs only); OpenAPI **59 paths** (untouched); **`actionlint` 1.7.12 CLEAN** (run locally — no CI workflow-linter exists) plus the planted static-red above. Per-artifact NUL/C0 scan CLEAN on every committed text blob (PIPE `git show`; the workflow YAML is printable-ASCII source); `node scripts/privacy-check.mjs` content leg run after the final commit, before push (workflow + docs; the role-ARN reference is a public-safe variable name, zero real values). ## M12 — Correctness arc (v2.1, from the M9-01 dogfood findings)

The v2.1 correctness arc, opened from the M9-01 second-round dogfood findings (confirmed items 1-4 in
the M9 findings append). Category-aware requirement assessment: the deterministic gap classifier stops
treating absence of evidence as a confirmed gap and stops laddering non-skill requirements through
skill evidence. Full house rigor - story records, planted-FAILs where a gate changes, CAS merges,
SEALs. Design authority: ADR-0020. Built ahead of M10 (Azure), which is fenced off until reset.

- **M12-01** · Docs refresh · **S** · `done` — README/PLAN reality pass (OpenAPI path count 45→58, fresh-install steps, bootstrap reset via `auth:sync-bootstrap`, v1/v2 status). Discharged M9-01 finding 5. PR #123 → main `4982f1d`; the M9-01 findings append is PR #124 → main `c284bee`.
- **M12-02** · Requirement-assessment taxonomy + category-aware classification + evaluator propagation · **L** (plan-mode class: schema + scoring semantics) · `done` — fixes M9-01 findings F1/F2/F3. AC: (1) `GAP_CLASSIFICATIONS` grows 5→8 (`unknown`, `satisfied_fact`, `not_applicable`), existing five frozen; additive nullable `gaps.evaluator` + `gaps.confidence` columns (migration 0023). (2) `classify-gaps.ts` routes on `requirement.category` before the skill ladder; seniority uses the shared `evaluateSeniorityThreshold` both the fit dimension and the classifier consume (F3), comp/location → `not_applicable`, administrative `other` → `unknown` via a plain-code phrase list. (3) `genuine_gap` requires a positive signal; no-signal → `unknown` (F1). (4) The three evidence-status classes never reach an LLM drafting payload (zero prompt changes) and are suppressed from learning surfaces; `unknown` carries a deterministic resolution affordance + deep link. (5) Market signal: `needsInputCount` + `needs_input` reason, `MARKET_SIGNAL_SCORER_VERSION` 1→2. (6) The two invariants green: an evaluator-satisfied requirement never emits `genuine_gap`; no-evidence-and-no-positive-signal ⇒ `unknown`. D-8 strength flip DEFERRED (blocked by the evidence-strength law; folds into the parked skill-model split - see ADR-0020). ADR-0020.
  *(BUILD RECORD, one PR, single `feat(M12-02)` commit — status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **Scope:** core vocab (8 classes + `GAP_EVALUATORS`/`GAP_CONFIDENCES` + `isEvidenceStatusClassification`), scoring (`evaluators/seniority-threshold.ts` extracted + shared by dimension and classifier, category-routed `classify-gaps.ts`, market-signal `needs_input`/`needsInputCount` + scorer version 1→2), db (schema `evaluator`/`confidence` + migration 0023 drop+recreate the classification CHECK to 8, no backfill — all five old values remain, no orphaning — + constraint pins verified against real PG), api (fit routes carry the new fields, OpenAPI regenerated 58 paths), llm (four drafting payload builders exclude the three evidence-status classes, ZERO prompt/registry changes), web (Gaps labels+LADDER for 8, an `unknown` resolution affordance + deep link, Skill Signal `needsInputCount`, CreateLearningPlan allow-list). **GATES (bare, unpiped for the recorded run):** `pnpm typecheck` clean (all packages); `pnpm lint` clean (eslint + `prettier --check`); root `pnpm test` **2226 passing / 200 files, exit 0**; per-artifact NUL/C0 scan on every committed blob (PIPE on `git show <sha>:<path>`, never capture) — clean; `privacy-check` content leg after the final commit, before push — PASS (114 distinctive tokens, zero real-profile strings). **No verification gate is modified in this story → NO planted-FAIL owed** (the gate-modification law's trigger is untouched; the new gaps CHECK pins are ordinary added test content, not a modified gate). **REVIEW SUBSTITUTE (Fable seat unavailable this window):** the mandatory ultracode adversarial panel over the full committed diff — four independent skeptic lenses (correctness / house-law / privacy-leak / plan-conformance), every finding refuter-verified. Correctness + house-law re-run after transient API 500s on the first pass. NET surviving findings, both fixed in the same change: (i) MINOR correctness — `ADMINISTRATIVE_PATTERNS` missed common variants ("authorization to work", "drug screening"/"drug testing"), routing them to `unknown`/`skill_evidence` (correct class, wrong resolution hint) → variants added + test cases; (ii) NIT — ADR-0020 admin phrase list was not in sync with the code const → reworded non-exhaustive, pointing at the const as authoritative. `/code-review high` (8 angles) afterward surfaced no new defect. Carlos reads the class-(a) diff (tests + schema) before the merge word. **DEVIATIONS (disclosed):** (a) **D-8 adjacent→direct strength flip DEFERRED** — the evidence-strength law (`fitReportDataSchema` refines "direct evidence requires a named profile skill") rejects a `direct` link with only a `profileExperienceId`; the honest strength change folds into the parked skill-model split (ADR-0020, consequence D-8). Surfaced to Carlos when the schema refine rejected the flip. (b) a market-signal **`needs_input` noAction reason** was added beyond the plan's `needsInputCount`-only sketch so an all-`unknown` group is never mislabeled "covered". (c) plans/learning/market-signal end-to-end fixtures gained positive-signal (learning-level) skills because the F1 fix reclassified their no-signal requirements from `genuine_gap` to `unknown`. (d) ADR number is **0020** (the arc draft's "0016" was stale; v2 added 0016–0019). **NEW TESTS:** category-routing matrix + two fast-check invariants (evaluator-satisfied never `genuine_gap`; no-signal ⇒ `unknown`), seniority-threshold units, market-signal `needs_input` cohort, a payload-exclusion leak guard across all four builders (parse + byte-stability), gaps CHECK constraint pins. **POST-MERGE real pass OWED** (operator, counts only): re-score the real corpus; expect the noise classes to move to `satisfied_fact`/`not_applicable`/`unknown`; distribution dispositioned in chat + appended (class (b)). **NEXT for close:** Carlos's explicit merge word → CAS `gh pr merge --match-head-commit <reviewed-head>` → SEAL verify (2-parent, tree-identical, ancestor, heads=main-only, 0 tags, branch pruned) → **SEALED 2026-07-29:** PR #125 CAS-merged on Carlos's explicit word ("merge it") with `--match-head-commit 3d0f985`; merge commit main `a1b80e8` (2-parent: old main `c284bee` + reviewed head `3d0f985`). SEAL verified firsthand (all five): tree-identical (`git diff --quiet 3d0f985 a1b80e8` exit 0), reviewed head ancestor-verified on main, remote branch auto-deleted, remote heads = main only, 0 tags. All 7 CI checks green on the pushed SHA (retired-name, gitleaks, private-profile-absent, test, typecheck, lint, portfolio-build). POST-MERGE real-corpus re-score still OWED (operator, counts only) - carried into M12-03.*

Migration 0024. Privacy-check gains a facts probe ⇒ **planted-FAIL owed** in the same change (facts are a sensitive class).
  *(2026-07-29 BUILD RECORD, one PR - status `done` on code-complete + all gates green; the merge/SEAL line is recorded separately at close, post-merge, per evidence-before-claims.)* **Scope:** core (`PROFILE_FACT_KINDS` six + graded stance sets + `profileFactSchema`/`profileFactImportSchema`, per-kind value refinement + NUL guards); db (`profile_facts` table + **migration 0024** CREATE-TABLE-only - kind CHECK, conditional value-vocab CHECK for the three closed-vocab kinds, `UNIQUE (user_id, kind)`; `createProfileFactsRepository` full-sync-in-one-tx + constraint pins); import (`facts-parser.ts` embedded-YAML mirror of criteria-parser; OPTIONAL absence-tolerant `facts.md`; full-sync by kind wired into `importProfile`; fictional `docs/profile.example/facts.md`); scoring (`evaluators/profile-fact.ts` + `classifyDurableFact` wired into the `other`/administrative route, `classifyGaps(input, facts=[])` parallel arg, location-arm stance clause); api (`GET /profile/facts`, OpenAPI 58 -> 59); web (Evidence Library "Declared facts" panel, `GapSection.vue` durable-fact resolution affordance). **ADR-0021.** **PRE-IMPLEMENTATION ADVERSARIAL DESIGN REVIEW (ultracode, the Fable-seat substitute this window):** four skeptic lenses over the approved PLAN + code - 20 findings raised, 14 refuter-refuted, **6 survived (2 blockers + 4 majors), ALL fixed before a line was written:** (BLOCKER-1) the visa-sponsorship conflict rule used negative-phrase detection that the token matcher (which keeps `no`/`not` distinct) could never match - "Visa sponsorship not available" (the repo's own fixture) would have become a silenced `satisfied_fact` for a needs-sponsorship candidate -> INVERTED to affirmative-only detection (fails toward `unknown`); (BLOCKER-2) free-form kinds emitted `satisfied_fact`/high on mere presence, fabricating a fit conclusion (an EU-authorized candidate marked "satisfied" on a US-citizenship requirement) -> now requires a POSITIVE determination (work_auth country corroboration, security_clearance never auto-satisfied); (majors) the phrase->kind map missed a committed spelling -> derived from one source list; the DB dropped a value CHECK on a false premise -> conditional CHECK added; `GapSection.vue` had no `durable_profile_fact` affordance -> added + component-tested; `classifyGaps` second arg defaulted so ~15 call sites stay green. A SECOND ultracode panel over the COMMITTED diff (pre-push) raised 7, refuted 2, **5 survived (2 blockers + 1 major + 2 minors), ALL fixed pre-push:** (BLOCKER) the first sponsorship fix's bare verb-object phrases ('offer/provide sponsorship') re-opened the negation hole - "we do NOT offer sponsorship" / "no sponsorship available" matched (a preceding negation does not break strict adjacency) -> added a NEGATION_CUES guard so any negation anywhere leaves the requirement `unknown`; (BLOCKER) the bare 'us' country alias collided with the English pronoun "us" ("join us today" read as the US country group, a false conflict/false-satisfy) -> dropped the ambiguous two-letter codes (us/uk/eu), corroboration now keys off spelled-out names + national adjectives (abbreviation-only => satisfied/medium, never a false conflict); (major) the privacy facts extractor was physical-line-only and missed multi-line YAML block/folded scalars -> continuation-line folding + a second multi-line planted-FAIL; (minors) a non-scalar `note` now raises a ParseIssue instead of silently nulling, and evidence.vue's facts fetch now renders an error state instead of a misleading "no facts declared". **R1 (Carlos-confirmed):** a fact NEVER produces `genuine_gap` - a conflict routes to `unknown`/"needs your input", never a skill-gap, never an exclusion (D-4). Carlos's fact-semantics direction folded in (work auth always yes for the US; needs no visa sponsorship; holds no security clearance). **GATES (bare, unpiped):** `pnpm typecheck` clean (all packages); `pnpm lint` clean (eslint + `prettier --check`); root `pnpm test` **2280 passing / 203 files, exit 0** (+54 over M12-02's 2226); per-artifact NUL/C0 scan on committed blobs (PIPE on `git show`, never capture) - clean, incl. a Write-tool raw-NUL struck in `core/profile.ts` (a `U+0000` escape became a raw byte; caught by a direct `perl` file scan after `grep|perl` was fooled by "Binary file matches"; fixed to `String.fromCharCode(0)`); `privacy-check` content leg after the final commit, before push. **Privacy gate MODIFIED -> planted-FAIL DEMONSTRATED in the same change:** a facts `value`/`note` extractor as a FOURTH `normalizedPasses` entry (whole-string, base-subtracted, NEVER consulting PUBLISHED - facts are a sensitive class); a fictional fact value leaks `(facts, normalized)` exit 1, neutering the pass turned EXACTLY that test red (expected +0 to be 1) with the other 10 green, restoring returned all green. **DEVIATIONS (disclosed):** (a) satisfied_fact is a thin surface by design - only visa_sponsorship (yes/no) and country-corroborated work_authorization auto-satisfy; security_clearance always routes to `unknown` (level comparison deferred, the honest choice). (b) country matching uses a small recognized-country alias set (documented ADR-0021 limitation). (c) `GET /profile` unchanged - facts get their own read route. **NEW TESTS:** evaluator matrix + mapping completeness + sponsorship affirmative/negative + I3 (facts never genuine_gap) + facts-never-reach-scoreFit; facts parser (valid/bad-kind/bad-stance/missing-file/control-byte); constraint pins (kind/value-vocab/UNIQUE/cascade); importer idempotency + full-sync delete + optional-file; core schema; a non-vacuous LLM-payload exclusion pin; the GapSection affordance. **NEXT for close:** Carlos's explicit merge word -> CAS `gh pr merge --match-head-commit <reviewed-head>` -> SEAL verify (2-parent, tree-identical, ancestor, heads=main-only, 0 tags, branch pruned) -> **SEALED (merged 2026-07-29, 00:57 UTC 07-30):** PR #126 CAS-merged on Carlos's explicit word ("Merge it now", chosen over hold-for-Saturday-Fable) with `--match-head-commit 224857b` (the FULL 40-char SHA; an abbreviated 7-char failed with a GraphQL "could not coerce" error); merge commit main `cdc5067` (2-parent: old main `a1b80e8` + reviewed head `224857b`). All 7 CI checks green on the pushed SHA (gitleaks, lint, portfolio-build, private-profile-absent, retired-name, test, typecheck). Migrations to 0024, OpenAPI 59, ADR-0021. The M12-02 POST-MERGE real-corpus re-score carries forward (operator, counts only).*

**M12-02 real-corpus re-score disposition (2026-08-01, operator-run; counts only, per the build record's owed post-merge pass).** Carlos re-scored his 7 open-application postings after the M12 arc merged AND the same-day profile enrichment (facts declared, criteria rewritten, skills 23->82, richer experience bullets). Apples-to-apples comparison - same 7 postings, identical extraction runs, 107 requirements in both eras, latest pre-arc report vs latest post-arc report per posting: `genuine_gap` fell **40 -> 2**, with the 38 departures exactly accounted for by the three new evidence-status classes - `unknown` 28 (the F1 no-signal fix), `not_applicable` 8 (comp/location routing), `satisfied_fact` 2 (durable-fact corroboration at high confidence: the declared work-authorization and sponsorship facts). Independently of the taxonomy, the profile enrichment moved ~6 requirements from `needs_refresh`/`low_priority` into `have_undemonstrated` (5 -> 11); `have` held exactly stable at 34. Evaluator distribution on the new reports: `skill_evidence` 86, `dimension_delegation` 11 (all high), `seniority_threshold` 8 (the F3 propagation live on real data), `durable_profile_fact` 2 (high). **Disposition: CONFIRMS the arc** - the noise classes moved precisely where the M12-02 plan predicted, no unexpected class movement, and the remaining 2 `genuine_gap`s are believed-real gaps. The arc's last owed item is discharged; nothing remains open on M12.

## M13 — External exam hardening (v2.1, from the EX-0002 exam findings)

Opened 2026-08-02 from an EXTERNAL review: the AI Code Clinic exam report **EX-0002** (Mary),
a read-only source exam of the public repo. Provenance caveats, recorded before any story runs:
the report's last page marks it **DRAFT — not reviewed, not signed** ("false positives not killed,
paths not verified"), and it was drafted mid-M10 — so every finding was independently re-verified
against main `9f68a41` (post-M10-07) on 2026-08-02 before disposition. All 12 findings (F-1..F-12)
end in a state per the disposition law: **10 → the nine stories below** (F-12 folds into
F-10's story), **1 → Icebox park** (F-9), **1 → dismissed with reason** (F-11). The exam's "what is fine" section
independently corroborates the standing posture (default-deny auth gate, per-user scoping with
unguessable ids, no v-html + the XSS e2e, argon2 + login rate limit + timing-equalized login,
provider-level spend cap, no live secrets in code or history) — no action items there.
Stories are listed in recommended execution order, not report order. House rigor applies
per story: plan mode where the story's class demands it, planted-FAIL wherever a gate is
created or modified, CAS merges, SEALs, privacy-check on profile-adjacent branches.

**Second-opinion cross-check (2026-08-02, folded before first review):** Carlos ran the exam
through an independent ChatGPT evaluation; it converged with the firsthand re-verification on
all 12 verdicts (same do-first pick, same park, same dismissal) and contributed engineering
detail now folded into the AC below — the `packages/db` boundary for the readiness probe,
atomic/outside-the-repo backup mechanics with the profile sources included, the
preview-fingerprint import contract, dependency-review-on-PR over per-PR audit noise, and
re-scoping the keyed-mode LLM budget as trigger-bound. Its one divergence (accept F-2 outright
vs decide-and-record) is resolved in favor of decide-and-record: the disposition law requires
the acceptance to land in RISKS.md, not in chat. **A third, adversarial pass over the merged
disposition itself (operator-directed) then caught what BOTH reviews missed, folded into the
AC below:** the public-allowlist and route-pin tests are named verification gates, so M13-04
and M13-08 owe planted-FAILs; a public readiness endpoint is an unauthenticated DB-roundtrip
amplifier (cache/throttle it) and its polling interacts with Neon scale-to-zero (cost + ALB
flap); an outside-the-repo backup destination is NOT off-disk (T-02's lost-laptop scenario
needs off-machine, encrypted in transit); bare `pnpm audit` fails on any severity without the
level knob; Dependabot cadence must respect the every-PR-needs-a-merge-word ceremony; the
NODE_ENV default removal blasts every `parseEnv`-consuming CLI, not just the server; and the
import-confirm contract must bind the HTTP ingress, not only the CLI. **Scope guardrail, both reviews agree:** this
arc is NOT a security rewrite — the exam-confirmed strengths (default-deny auth gate, per-user
scoping with indistinguishable miss/foreign lookups, parameterized Drizzle access, escape-only
rendering + the XSS e2e, argon2id + login limiting + timing-equalized login, secret scanning +
profile guards, the keyless-demo invariant, the provider-level spend cap) are preserved
verbatim; no story may broaden into auth/session/ORM/sanitization redesign.

- **M13-01** · Backup & restore runbook + `db:backup` (F-5, exam "Data you cannot get back") · **M** · `done` — the exam's top operational finding, verified real: RISKS.md T-02 (:19, :88) claims "scheduled `pg_dump`… restore procedure documented and tested once" as its mitigation, but RUNBOOKS.md contains **no backup or restore section at all**, and the only copy of the real profile/application data is the local `pgdata` docker volume (one `docker compose down -v` from gone; ADR-0015 local-first makes this THE single point of loss — the M10 demo's Neon DB holds fictional data only and is no mitigation). AC: (1) `pnpm db:backup` producing a dated **custom-format** `pg_dump` (run via the compose container's own pg_dump — client/server version match) to a configured private destination OUTSIDE the repo AND **off the primary disk** (external drive or cloud — a same-disk copy survives a wrong `down -v` but NOT T-02's actual disk-failure/lost-laptop scenario; anything leaving the machine is encrypted) — the script FAILS if the destination resolves inside the repo, writes to a temp file and renames atomically only after `pg_dump` succeeds, restricts file permissions, and never prints credentials, row content, or private-value filenames; (2) the same run backs up `docs/profile/` alongside the DB — the gitignored markdown is equally authoritative and equally unrecoverable; (3) a RUNBOOKS.md "Backup & restore" section — exact commands, schedule, retention, restore steps, failure handling; (4) the scheduled job (launchd/cron) it describes actually installed; (5) `pnpm db:restore:verify` restoring into a DISPOSABLE database/compose project (NEVER `down -v` against the real project or real `pgdata`), row counts compared, cleaned up after; (6) **one real restore drill performed by Carlos**, date recorded — evidence-before-claims, the T-02 "tested once" claim becomes true only after the drill runs, never before; (7) RISKS.md T-02 row updated to cite the runbook section. `.gitignore` already excludes dump/backup patterns — keep or strengthen, never weaken.

AC: (1) `/health` stays pure process liveness (ADR-0007 allowlist entry unchanged) and container/ECS liveness keeps pointing at it — a DB blip must not make the orchestrator kill/restart healthy processes; (2) a new public `/health/ready` returns 200 when a `SELECT 1`-class ping succeeds under a short timeout, 503 with a sanitized body (no internals) when it doesn't — **the ping lives in `packages/db`** (a `checkDbReady(timeout)` repository-level function; no SQL outside packages/db, the module-boundary law); (3) `packages/core` wire mirror extended; OpenAPI + route-list pin + public-allowlist tests updated — allowlist growth = plan-mode class (auth-adjacent), AND the **allowlist test is a named verification gate** (CLAUDE.md), so its modification owes a **demonstrated planted-FAIL** in the same change; (4) the endpoint is unauthenticated and does a DB roundtrip → cap the amplification: cache the readiness verdict for ~1-2s (or throttle) so a public hammer costs one ping per window, not one per request; (5) external uptime monitoring + the M10-08 go-live smoke point at readiness; Terraform reviewed for which probe each surface consumes, **with the Neon interaction decided explicitly**: a monitor polling readiness defeats Neon scale-to-zero (permanent compute = real money) and a cold-resume 503 would flap an ALB pointed at readiness — so ALB/ECS stay on `/health` liveness and readiness polling frequency is a deliberate, costed choice; (6) verified with injected readiness failure or a disposable DB — never by stopping the real dev database mid-work.

- **M13-06** · CSRF Origin posture: decide and record (F-2, exam "What the app trusts") · **S** · `done` — verified as designed, not a bug: `auth.hooks.ts:66` rejects a mutating request only when Origin is present AND mismatched; absent-Origin requests proceed to the session check, per the documented "non-browser clients are outside the CSRF threat model" comment. Browsers always send Origin cross-site, so browser CSRF is covered; the residual is a cookie-bearing non-browser client omitting the header. The exam asks the owner to confirm the tradeoff — this story is that decision, Carlos's call: EITHER (a) require Origin on mutating routes (first confirming the SPA, e2e, and smoke flows all send it) OR (b) record the acceptance as an explicit RISKS.md entry naming the threat model, the compensating controls (SameSite=Lax, single user, HttpOnly session cookie), and a revisit trigger — **re-open mandatory on any public, keyed, or multi-user deployment**. New `auth.origin-required.test.ts` (6 cases) + a drift-proof `ORIGIN_HEADER` helper in auth-test-helpers.ts. - **M13-09** · Profile-import deletion guard (F-7, exam "Data you cannot get back") · **S** · `done` — verified as designed: the importer full-syncs (D-4, "the file is the source of truth" — `profile.service.ts:204`), so a truncated/mis-edited markdown silently deletes DB rows, and gitignored `docs/profile/` has no history to recover from. The full-mirror semantics STAY (both reviews agree — no append-only drift); the guard is preview-then-confirm: (1) a `--dry-run`/preview pass parses all sources and reports add/update/**delete** counts WITHOUT writing, returning a source fingerprint; (2) an import that would delete rows requires explicit confirmation (`--confirm-deletes` or interactive) carrying that fingerprint, and is REJECTED (409-style) if the files changed between preview and execution — the repo's own compare-and-swap idiom applied to the importer — and the contract binds BOTH ingress surfaces: the CLI and `POST /profile/import` (`profile.routes.ts:117-171`), which takes the fingerprint in its body (an HTTP caller must not bypass what the CLI enforces); (3) non-destructive imports proceed directly, `--example` imports and tests bypass the prompt (fictional data); (4) `pnpm profile:import` also snapshots the real `docs/profile/` to a dated private location OUTSIDE the repo before any destructive sync (complements M13-01, which owns scheduled backups — **ordering: M13-01 lands first**); (5) logs and responses stay value-free — counts and fingerprints only, never profile text. Touches the real-profile path → privacy-check leg mandatory; snapshots never enter the repo or logs.

## Parked (process/tooling)

- **Evidence-binding (named class, not a park — no trigger)** *(2026-07-15, M1-03 kickoff)*: evidence names its object — a SHA, a port, an artifact — or it isn't evidence. Instances to date: the M0-10 squatter (green banner, wrong server → port preflight + banner-marker checks) and the PR #11 merge race (green checks, wrong commit → the pin-the-head rule, promoted to CLAUDE.md law on the M1-03 branch, in atomic form: verify headRefOid == pushed SHA, then merge with `--match-head-commit`). The pin-the-head rule is this class's second instance, not a one-off; future "X is green" claims must state which object X was observed on.
- **Privacy gate matching semantics** *(parked 2026-07-15, M1-01 kickoff)*: two narrowings shipped to date (added-lines-only, M0-09; lockfile exclusion, M0-10) — each sound in isolation, but the trend is category exclusions for what is really a matching-semantics problem: short real-profile tokens substring-colliding with public strings. **Trigger:** on the NEXT false positive, the fix is word-boundary or distinctiveness-based matching in `scripts/privacy-check.mjs`, not a fourth exclusion. (Gate-modification law applies: any such change ships with a demonstrated FAIL on planted fictional data.) **→ RESOLVED (2026-07-15, same branch, hours later):** the trigger fired on M1-01's own gate run (see the M1-01 FINDING disposition) and the fix is distinctiveness-based as prescribed — tokens already present in the base branch's committed content are the repo's public vocabulary and are subtracted from the probe set, generalizing the existing example-corpus subtraction under the same already-public logic as added-lines-only. Word-boundary matching was the wrong tool for the actual instance (it was a whole-word match). Probe evidence in the ledger. **ADDENDUM (2026-07-15, M1-02 branch — the queued ratchet review):** three properties of the distinctiveness fix, recorded on second look. (1) **The subtraction is a ratchet, and self-blinding after a miss:** base vocabulary only grows — once a string exists in the base tree the gate permanently stops probing it, so if a real-profile token ever lands on main (through this gate or around it), every future branch republishing it scans clean. Accepted, not fixed: a base-tree occurrence is by definition a pre-existing incident whose remedy is rotation/history-purge (RISKS S-03 procedure), not per-branch detection; the compensating controls that do NOT subtract stay in force (gitleaks pre-commit + CI, the tracked-file guard, and the added-lines scan itself for every token not yet in base). (2) **The M0-10 lockfile exclusion is NOT subsumed by base-vocabulary subtraction** — implementer corrects reviewer here, and the kickoff-review "subsumed" framing is explicitly retired (the falsification loop now runs in every direction): subtraction covers only tokens already in the base tree, while installing a NEW dependency adds registry identifiers absent from base — dropping the exclusion would recreate the exact M0-10 substring-collision class on the next dependency bump. Both mechanisms stay. (3) **`git grep -I`** (script change riding this addendum): without `-I`, binary files in the base tree contribute `Binary file <path> matches` lines to the subtraction corpus, letting file PATHS blind the gate. Gate-modification law satisfied by scratch-repo probes (planted fictional token throughout): a binary named with the token made the pre-change script MISS the token when a branch added it in text (exit 0 — demonstrated self-blinding) while the `-I` script detects it (exit 1); plain planted-token detection 1→1 (kept); clean branch 0→0.

- **`github-pages` environment admin-bypass vs the empty ruleset bypass** *(parked 2026-07-20, M2-01 close; docs-codification batch — document, do not change now)*: GitHub auto-created the `github-pages` deployment environment (M2-01, ADR-0008) with "Allow administrators to bypass configured protection rules" **enabled**, while the `master-protection` ruleset carries an **empty** bypass list (CLAUDE.md's deliberate friction). The two are inconsistent in posture but the environment setting **governs nothing today** — no environment reviewers or wait timer are configured, so there is no protection to bypass. **Trigger:** if environment protection (required reviewers / wait timer / stricter branch policy) is ever added to `github-pages`, revisit the admin-bypass toggle so it matches the empty-bypass posture. No environment secrets or variables exist.

- **Test-isolation hardening (shared-`careerforge_test` truncation-lock flake)** *(parked 2026-07-23, M2-10 close — a real priority, will recur)*: the integration suite shares one `careerforge_test` DB and each affected `beforeEach` calls `truncateAllTables`, which now takes an ACCESS-EXCLUSIVE lock on 22 tables (M2-10's migration 0008 added 4) while the run also gained 2 DB-integration files. On M2-10's final head, `test` went RED once (`gaps.repository.test.ts` + `extraction.injection.routes.test.ts`, both timing out at the truncate `beforeEach` at ~20s = lock-wait, **no assertion failed**), then re-ran GREEN — a non-deterministic contention race, not a correctness defect. **Trigger:** the next time this flake blocks a merge (or on any deliberate CI-stability pass). **Candidate fixes:** cap the pg pool `max` (currently unset = pg default 10) for the app/api DB phase, and/or serialize that phase with `sequence.groupOrder` the way `packages/db/vitest.config.ts` already pins `fileParallelism:false` + `groupOrder:1`. Not M2-10 feature scope.

- **`.claude/` per-seat config tripping local `prettier --check .`** *(2026-07-28, resolved same-day; env finding surfaced by B2, relayed as a Carlos directive via the ceremony seat; disposition (a) fixed)*: each seat's worktree now carries an untracked `.claude/settings.local.json` (per-seat permission enforcement, PROTOCOL 2026-07-28), which bare `pnpm lint` (`eslint . && prettier --check .`) flagged in EVERY worktree because prettier does not read `.gitignore` by default and `.claude/` was absent from `.prettierignore`. **CI unaffected** (CI checkouts carry no untracked per-seat file). **Resolved (class (a) gate-config, this PR):** added `.claude/` to the root `.prettierignore`. Demonstrated detection (gate-modification law): bare `prettier --check .` captured RED (exit 1, `.claude/settings.local.json`) before and GREEN (exit 0) after; then, to prove the ignore is scoped and not a blanket disable, a planted formatting violation in a tracked source file (`apps/portfolio/app/utils/provenance.ts`) STILL failed `prettier --check .` (exit 1, named the file) and was reverted. One ignore line + comment; NO source, gate-script, or `lighthouserc.cjs` change.

## Icebox (explicitly deferred)

- **M3-05a · Spacing-enforced revisit graduation** *(M3-05, 2026-07-25 — named at the plan gate, Carlos-ratified park)*: M3-05 graduation is COUNT-indexed — three `revisited` records graduate an exercise regardless of when they happened (three consecutive days works), consistent with the evidence-is-trusted stance. The refinement — a revisit counts toward the ladder only if it was recorded on/after its due date — is deliberate future work, not an oversight: it adds write-adjacent policy (which revisits "count") that an S story should not mint. Trigger: real dogfood evidence that same-week revisit stacking is actually happening and diluting the ladder. Pure `computeRevisitState` change + tests; no schema.

- **M3-06a · Evidence recency bound on upgrade suggestions** *(M3-06, 2026-07-25 — named at the plan gate, Carlos-ratified park; ADR-0014 OD-6)*: M3-06 re-derives suggestions timelessly — declare a skill `rusty` today and an exercise completed months ago immediately re-suggests upgrading it (stale evidence meeting a freshly-declared-stale skill). Accepted for now: the confirmation gate is the filter (Carlos sees the exercise's `completedOn` in the suggestion and simply doesn't confirm). The refinement — a `completedOn` recency bound below which an exercise stops suggesting — is a new policy knob an S story should not mint (the M3-05a lesson verbatim). Trigger: dogfood evidence that stale-evidence suggestions are actually misleading in practice. Pure `suggestSkillUpgrades` change + tests; no schema.
- **Cross-plan gap→exercises read** *(M3-02, 2026-07-24 — named at the plan gate)*: M3-02 satisfies "a gap shows its exercises" **only within the plan being viewed** (the `GET /learning-plans/:id` embed). A gap can be cited by several plans (`gaps }o--o{ learning_plans`), so a gap-centric read that lists ALL exercises addressing it across every plan is a distinct capability with no home today (no gap-detail endpoint exists). Trigger: when a gap-first view of skill work is actually wanted. No new schema needed (query through `exercise_gaps`); it is an API/read-surface story.
- Automated job collection (legal guardrails per RISKS.md L-01 are prerequisites, plus an ADR)
- Resume tailoring/export per posting — **PROMOTED 2026-07-19** (Q5 decided at the M1 retro) → story M2-10; no longer iceboxed
- Multi-user support, hosted platform, email/calendar integration, browser extension for one-click paste *(2026-07-26, M4-04 — annotation: **hosted platform** was DECIDED by ADR-0015 — stay local-first, defer public platform deployment, with four named reopening triggers; it leads the "## v2 backlog" section below as a recorded candidate (not iceboxed-indefinitely). Multi-user and email/calendar are likewise promoted to named v2 candidates there; the browser-extension one-click paste stays iceboxed. Original text retained.)*
